### [79] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向大语言模型（LLM）分解式推理场景的拓扑感知KV缓存传输编排器，通过感知GPU间互连层级差异（NVLink/InfiniBand/TCP）选择最优传输路径，并利用流水线分层传输、MoE专家放置和CXL 3.0内存扩展器来降低prefill与decode分离时的KV缓存传输延迟。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）分解式推理的数据中心网络传输问题，核心对象是KV cache在GPU池之间的传输，属于分布式推理/数据中心网络系统方向。与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感任务；也不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化方法。唯一可能的间接关联是"拓扑感知传输调度"和"流水线重叠计算与通信"这类系统优化思想，以及CXL/NVLink等硬件加速概念，但这些与在轨遥感目标检测的轻量化部署场景差异极大。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，属于LLM分布式推理系统方向。除非对星上异构计算资源调度或计算-通信重叠有特殊兴趣，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM分解式推理（disaggregated LLM inference）中的KV cache跨GPU池传输调度。应用场景：数据中心/GPU集群网络，非遥感或卫星场景。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 提出一个拓扑感知传输编排器（topology-aware transfer orchestrator），包含三个核心机制：(1) 流水线分层传输（pipelined layer-by-layer transfer），将KV cache按层切分并重叠传输与prefill计算；(2) NVLink domain-aware placement，针对Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型联合优化expert dispatch与KV cache局部性；(3) CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6x容量且延迟比NVMe低86x。系统在启动时发现互连层级（NVLink 900GB/s、InfiniBand 50GB/s、TCP 12.5GB/s），为每次传输选择最优传输协议。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化方法。论文不包含剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速技术。其优化对象是系统层面的数据传输调度与硬件拓扑感知，而非模型本身的参数或计算量压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。论文面向数据中心GPU集群场景，涉及NVLink、InfiniBand、TCP、CXL 3.0等数据中心级互连硬件，与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）场景完全不同。论文提到完整评估需要多节点异构互连集群和CXL 3.0硬件，超出学术资源且GPU云尚不可用，因此仅提供分析性带宽模型、组件实现和投影分析。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等与在轨部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为KV cache传输延迟（transfer latency reduction），报告相对uniform RDMA有3-18x的传输延迟降低。涉及70B模型每请求2.6GB KV cache、生产规模下超过100GB/s聚合带宽等规模参数。未使用任何遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标（如mAP、FPS等）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明完整实验细节。由于CXL 3.0硬件和异构互连集群不可用，论文仅提供分析性带宽模型（analytical bandwidth models）、组件实现（component implementations）和跨三种架构的投影分析（projected analysis），显示相对uniform RDMA有3-18x传输延迟降低。未提供与遥感目标检测方法的对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 主要局限：(1) 完整评估受限于硬件不可用，仅能提供分析模型和投影结果，缺乏真实系统验证；(2) 场景完全面向数据中心LLM推理，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联；(3) 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术；(4) 不涉及遥感数据、目标检测任务或星上部署约束（功耗、算力、存储限制）。

Ideas for my research: 尽管该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但仍有少量可借鉴的系统级思想：(1) "流水线分层传输重叠计算与通信"的思想可类比到星上推理中模型分层推理与数据传输的重叠调度，例如在轨多任务推理时重叠图像采集与推理计算；(2) "拓扑感知调度"思想可类比到星上多核/多NPU异构计算资源的任务调度，根据计算单元间的带宽差异优化数据流；(3) "分层存储（CXL/NVMe）"思想可类比到星上存储层级设计（SRAM/DRAM/Flash）的优化。但这些借鉴均属于系统级优化思路，对模型轻量化本身（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）帮助有限。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [80] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶系统（ADS）识别问题，利用车辆遥测数据（telematics data）通过GRU、LSTM和Transformer编码器三种序列分类模型来区分不同的Level 2自动驾驶系统（Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise）与人工驾驶，并提出了一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架来模拟遥测数据退化。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向（遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化）基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶系统识别（ADS identification），使用车辆遥测数据（telematics data）进行序列分类，既不涉及遥感/卫星/Earth observation，也不涉及目标检测（object detection），更不涉及轻量化（lightweight）或边缘/星上部署（onboard deployment）。属于泛机器学习/时序分类应用，相关性评分为1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为自动驾驶系统识别，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或模型压缩/加速。仅其鲁棒性评估框架的模块化设计思路可作为边缘参考，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶系统识别（ADS identification），即通过遥测数据判断当前激活的是哪一套Level 2自动驾驶系统（Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Super Cruise）还是人工驾驶。应用场景：自动驾驶安全监控、法规合规、保险评估、异常检测。传感器类型：车辆遥测数据（vehicle telematics data），包括连续通道（continuous channels）和二元事件信号（binary event signals）。目标类别：4类（3种ADS + 人工驾驶）。

Model architecture: 三种序列分类模型：1) Gated Recurrent Units (GRU)；2) Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks；3) Transformer encoder model。均为序列建模架构，用于处理时间序列遥测数据。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（如pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等），也未讨论模型参数量、FLOPs或推理延迟等部署相关指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论模型在嵌入式设备、车载或星上平台的部署可行性，未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：车辆遥测数据（vehicle telematics data），具体数据集名称摘要未说明。评价指标：macro F1-score（用于不平衡多分类评估）。实验设置：1) 干净数据训练（clean-data training）；2) 威胁匹配训练（threat-matched training）；3) 五种退化族（corruption families）× 五个严重级别（L1-L5）的鲁棒性评估，包括加性高斯白噪声+累积漂移、相关跨通道噪声、时间抖动（temporal jitter）、突发丢失、延迟转换、虚假切换和跨特征不一致等扰动。

Experiments: 干净数据训练下macro F1：GRU 0.92、LSTM 0.90、Transformer encoder 0.93。威胁匹配训练下macro F1为0.904-0.916，仅带来轻微干净数据性能损失。鲁棒性评估发现明显失效模式分裂：事件级退化（event-level corruptions）在L5级别仍保持macro F1 ≥ 0.87，而时间抖动（temporal jitter）使三种模型的macro F1骤降至0.44-0.50。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关；2) 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算复杂度、推理速度等部署相关信息；3) 摘要未说明具体数据集规模、来源和划分方式；4) 摘要未说明模型在真实车载环境中的部署验证情况；5) 时间抖动导致的性能崩溃（macro F1降至0.44-0.50）是明显的鲁棒性短板，但摘要未给出缓解方案。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测无直接关系，但其鲁棒性评估框架的设计思路有一定借鉴价值：1) 模块化的corruption families × severity levels评估范式可以迁移到遥感图像退化模拟（如噪声、模糊、遮挡、辐射畸变等）中，用于评估在轨目标检测模型在真实星上环境下的鲁棒性；2) 时间抖动（temporal jitter）导致性能崩溃的发现提示：在轨视频/序列目标检测中，时间对齐和帧间一致性是值得关注的鲁棒性维度；3) threat-matched training（威胁匹配训练）的思想可类比为在遥感场景下针对特定退化类型（如SAR噪声、云遮挡）进行针对性数据增强训练。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [81] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面分析基于动力系统（DS）的LLM响应分类方法，证明其误分类概率随序列长度指数衰减，并刻画了跨嵌入模型的泛化条件。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM）响应分类的理论分析，属于自然语言处理与动力系统理论的交叉领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM响应分类（binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs）；场景：AI系统分析；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 将token embeddings建模为黑盒动力系统（black-box dynamical system）的轨迹，通过比较两个DS的prediction residuals进行分类。理论框架为两个随机线性动力系统（stochastic linear DSs）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。理论指标包括：total variation distance、misclassification probability、dynamical discriminability δ²、intertwining map's smallest singular value。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验。论文主要提供理论证明：1) 忽略token dynamics的分类器存在accuracy floor；2) DS-based分类的误分类概率随序列长度L指数衰减，衰减率由δ²决定；3) 通过approximate intertwining condition刻画跨embedding模型的泛化下界。

Limitations: 本文为纯理论分析，未提供实验验证细节；研究对象是LLM响应分类，与遥感目标检测完全无关；理论框架（随机线性DS）难以直接迁移到视觉目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极为有限。唯一可能的思想火花是：将序列/时序特征建模为动力系统轨迹并利用残差进行判别，这一思路在理论上或许可启发遥感视频/时序目标检测中的时序建模，但跨度很大，实际可操作性低。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [82] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LARA（Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation），一种在冻结模型残差流上添加低秩修正的轻量适配方法，与LoRA在同等参数量下性能相当，并支持多行为在单一模型上常驻与按token自动路由。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/大语言模型参数高效微调（PEFT）方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、在轨部署均无直接关联。其"轻量适配"思想（在冻结基座模型上添加低秩残差模块）在方法论层面可能对轻量化部署有间接借鉴意义，但场景、任务、数据均与遥感目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 2分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，属于LLM参数高效微调范畴。其"冻结基座+低秩残差"和"多行为常驻路由"思想有一定方法论借鉴价值，但需大量改造才能迁移到遥感检测场景，优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：参数高效微调（PEFT）与偏好优化（DPO）；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）的代码微调与多行为托管；传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）；目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: LARA在冻结模型的残差流（residual stream）上操作，而非在权重矩阵上。它在少量层的隐藏状态处读取并添加低秩修正回残差流，所有基座权重保持不变。与LoRA（在权重矩阵上添加低秩更新）形成对比。模型基座为1.5B参数的冻结LLM，每个行为对应一个小的残差模块。

Lightweight method: 核心轻量化思想：冻结基座模型 + 低秩残差适配模块。每个行为（behavior）仅需约33MB开销（7个行为共享一个1.5B冻结模型），相比每个行为单独训练一个完整模型大幅节省存储。推理时通过scale γ在基座与适配行为之间平滑插值，实现分级控制。多个行为可常驻内存并按token自动路由，无需按需加载。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明具体部署硬件（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）。但"多个行为常驻单一设备模型"的托管方式对边缘/星上部署有潜在启发：共享冻结基座、按需路由小模块可显著降低存储与切换开销。摘要未提供参数量（除1.5B基座和33MB开销）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。任务包括代码微调任务和偏好优化（DPO）。评价指标为与LoRA在同等参数量下的性能对比（匹配LoRA性能）。

Experiments: 主要实验：在代码微调任务和DPO偏好优化上与LoRA对比，LARA在同等参数量下达到与LoRA相当的性能。在1.5B冻结模型上放置7个行为（6个微调+1个偏好优化），总开销约33MB。验证了scale γ的平滑插值能力和按token自动路由多行为的能力。

Limitations: 1. 场景为LLM微调，与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 摘要未说明在视觉/遥感任务上的适用性；3. 未提供计算量（FLOPs）、推理延迟、显存占用等部署关键指标；4. 未说明低秩残差适配在卷积网络或检测头等结构上的可行性；5. 1.5B基座模型规模对星上部署而言仍然偏大。

Ideas for my research: 1. 借鉴"冻结基座+低秩残差适配"思想：在遥感目标检测中，可考虑冻结预训练骨干（如ResNet/Swin），仅训练轻量低秩适配模块，减少星上需更新的参数量；2. "多行为常驻+按需路由"思想可迁移到多传感器（光学/SAR/高光谱）或多任务（检测/分割/场景理解）共享一个骨干的场景，降低星上存储与切换开销；3. scale γ的平滑插值机制可用于在轨模型的自适应推理，根据任务难度或资源约束动态调节适配强度；4. 但需注意：该方法是针对Transformer/LLM的残差流设计，迁移到CNN检测器需重新设计适配位置与形式。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [83] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K (CGA) 方法，通过高斯 copula 和 antithetic 构造在保持每个 token 的 routing law 不变的前提下，控制 MoE 模型中不同 token 路由选择的联合分布，从而在专家负载方差与组内一致性之间进行权衡。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/深度学习领域，聚焦 mixture-of-experts (MoE) 模型的路由机制与负载均衡，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化/压缩/部署。其相关性仅在于 MoE 路由机制可能对大规模模型的高效推理有间接启发，但与本课题"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文聚焦 MoE 路由分布的数学建模，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何压缩/部署技术，仅可作为泛机器学习背景阅读，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：MoE 模型的路由分布建模与负载均衡控制。应用场景：通用大规模语言/视觉模型的 mixture-of-experts 训练与推理。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 基于 mixture-of-experts (MoE) 模型，核心是 Gumbel-Top-K router。提出 Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K (CGA)：在组内用 exchangeable Gaussian copula 对 Gumbel perturbations 施加正相关以增强 expert-set coherence；在跨组间用 tunable antithetic 构造引入负相关。基础模型保持不动，仅通过一个小型 controller 在 frozen features 上驱动两个 dependence dials，使用 score-function estimator 训练。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统轻量化方法（pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, NAS 等均未涉及）。论文的"轻量"体现在：基础模型冻结，仅训练一个小型 controller，梯度只作用于 controller，frozen network 仅前向推理。这与模型压缩/加速不同，更多是训练效率层面的设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向 MoE 训练阶段的路由分布控制，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。仅提及"initial small-scale pilot"验证了机制和训练路径，但未给出数据集名称、评价指标或实验设置细节。

Experiments: 仅进行了 initial small-scale pilot 验证，确认了机制可行性和 score-function estimator 训练路径有效，但摘要明确说明"does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains"，即未验证任务级微调收益。

Limitations: 1. 摘要未说明具体实验规模、数据集和任务级效果，仅停留在机制验证层面；2. 未证明 task-level fine-tuning 的收益；3. 与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化/部署完全无关；4. 未提供任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但有两个潜在启发点：(1) "frozen backbone + 小型可训练 controller" 的范式，若未来在遥感目标检测中采用 MoE 结构，可考虑冻结主干、仅训练轻量路由控制器的思路以降低训练开销；(2) 负载均衡与 expert 使用率控制的思想，若在星上部署多分支/多专家检测模型时，可借鉴其负载分散控制思路。但总体而言，该论文与本课题方向关联度很低，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [84] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAWFUL框架，用于验证神经网络是否真正学习并内部使用物理定律（如多普勒频率定律），通过覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、有效域测试等可解释性工具，在Mocap2Radar transformer上验证其是否学习并应用Doppler定律。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。该论文聚焦于神经网络可解释性（interpretability）和物理定律学习验证，属于科学机器学习（scientific ML）与可解释AI领域。虽然涉及radar数据，但并非遥感卫星成像（如SAR/光学遥感），也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及任何轻量化或部署方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于可解释AI/科学机器学习领域。除非对物理先验注入检测模型的可解释性验证有特殊兴趣，否则不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：物理定律学习验证与神经网络可解释性分析。应用场景：从motion-capture和radar数据中验证transformer是否学习Doppler频率定律。传感器类型：radar（雷达，非遥感卫星SAR）。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，关注的是物理量（速度v(t)和频率f(t)）的因果流动。

Model architecture: Mocap2Radar transformer——一个将motion-capture数据映射到radar数据的transformer模型。论文提出LAWFUL框架，包含覆盖感知的因果一致性度量（coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals）和有效域测试（domain-of-validity test）等可解释性工具。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用motion-capture和radar数据，其中f(t)和v(t)均未显式出现在数据中。评价指标为覆盖感知的因果一致性度量（coverage-aware causal-consistency measure）和有效域测试（domain-of-validity test）。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Mocap2Radar transformer上验证其是否学习并内部使用Doppler频率定律 f(t) = 2v(t)/λ。摘要未给出具体数值实验结果或对比方法。

Limitations: 论文本身聚焦可解释性，与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关。摘要中提到的四个可解释性缺口中有两个（定律不变性与禁止行为验证、物理量流动量化）仅"奠定基础"而未完全解决。此外，该框架仅针对连续变量的物理定律，适用范围有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来在遥感目标检测中引入物理先验（如SAR成像几何、多普勒效应、运动学约束）来增强检测模型的可解释性和泛化能力，LAWFUL的因果一致性验证思路或许可作为评估物理约束是否被模型真正利用的工具。但这属于远期、间接的借鉴，与本课题核心（轻量化目标检测）无直接关系。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [85] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），通过双层优化实现自适应图划分以发现受试者特异性的脑功能模块，并利用这些模块作为先验指导边细化和表示学习，用于阿尔茨海默病（AD）分类。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。该论文聚焦于fMRI脑功能连接分析和阿尔茨海默病分类，属于神经影像学与图神经网络（GNN）交叉领域。既不涉及遥感/卫星/地球观测，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及轻量化或边缘部署。虽然使用了GNN和自适应图划分，但这是面向脑网络的图学习方法，与遥感在轨目标检测的视觉任务、传感器类型和部署约束均无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于脑影像图学习领域。除非对GNN自适应图划分或双层优化框架有跨领域借鉴兴趣，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：脑疾病分类（阿尔茨海默病AD分类）。场景：fMRI脑功能连接分析。传感器类型：fMRI（功能性磁共振成像），非遥感传感器。目标类别：AD患者 vs 健康对照。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆等）完全无关。

Model architecture: MPP-GNN（Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN）：采用双层优化（bilevel optimization）框架，包含自适应图划分（adaptive graph partitioning）以发现受试者特异性功能模块，并将发现的脑模块作为显式先验（explicit prior）指导边细化（edge refinement）和表示学习。属于图神经网络架构，与CNN/Transformer等视觉检测网络无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS或轻量网络设计等模型压缩/加速方法。其核心贡献是图划分与模块先验引导，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理能力。且fMRI分析本身属于离线医学影像处理场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：两个公开AD分类数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：AUC（Area Under the Curve）。对比方法：established baselines（具体基线名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在两个公开AD数据集上取得最高AUC，优于既有基线。此外，分析表明MPP-GNN与Yeo脑图谱定义的经典功能网络组织显著对齐，并揭示了AD的网络级去分化（dedifferentiation）模式。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：1）仅针对脑功能连接数据，泛化到视觉/遥感任务需大幅改造；2）未报告计算开销和部署指标；3）双层优化训练复杂度可能较高；4）未涉及实时推理或资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 尽管整体弱相关，但有两个方法论层面的思路可借鉴：1）"自适应图划分 + 模块先验引导边细化"的思想，若类比到遥感场景，可考虑将场景中的目标关系建模为图，用自适应聚类/划分来发现目标间的结构先验，辅助检测头优化；2）"双层优化"框架可用于遥感轻量化模型的联合优化（如同时优化剪枝策略与检测精度）。但这些借鉴较为间接，且需要大量改造才能迁移到遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [86] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于老年人认知障碍（MCI到痴呆）早期检测与管理的跨学科综述，涵盖EEG、神经影像、血液生物标志物和数字标记，并通过AI/ML/DL进行整合。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。论文主题是老年认知障碍的医学检测与健康管理，涉及EEG、MRI/PET神经影像、血液生物标志物等，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解；也不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等轻量化与部署技术。唯一勉强相关的点是文中提到深度学习模型（CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG foundation models），但这是泛医学AI应用，与遥感目标检测和轻量化方向无实质关联。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为老年认知障碍医学检测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：认知障碍（MCI→痴呆）的早期检测与健康管理综述。应用场景：老年医学临床筛查、神经退行性疾病诊断。传感器类型：EEG（脑电图）、MRI/PET神经影像、血液生物标志物、可穿戴设备、语音/VR数字标记。目标类别：认知障碍患者（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 摘要提及的模型包括：CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG foundation models，以及多模态融合框架。但均为医学信号/影像处理模型，与遥感目标检测网络架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。文中未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及的数据多为医学临床数据集，且指出许多研究基于small, single-site datasets，缺乏外部验证。评价指标涉及accuracy、sensitivity、specificity（医学诊断指标），与遥感检测的mAP、IoU、FPS等指标无关。

Experiments: 本文为综述性论文，无具体实验。主要贡献为：跨学科分类体系（taxonomy）、方法学严谨性视角（强调subject- and site-independent validation）、整合性早期检测框架、检测方法与干预措施对比表。提及血浆p-tau217达到临床可用性、抗淀粉样疗法（lecanemab, donanemab）获批等医学进展。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关。摘要本身指出该领域存在标准化、可解释性、数据隐私、公平性和外部验证部署等障碍，但这些是医学AI的挑战，与遥感在轨目标检测无关。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的启发是：文中强调"subject- and site-independent validation"（跨站点/跨主体验证），这可以类比为遥感领域强调跨传感器、跨场景、跨时相的泛化验证——即轻量化模型不应只在单一数据集上验证，而应在多源遥感数据（不同卫星、不同分辨率、不同波段）上验证其泛化性与压缩后的性能保持。此外，多模态融合思路（EEG+影像+血液+数字标记）可类比遥感中的多模态融合（光学+SAR+高光谱），但与本课题核心方向关联很弱。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [87] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEDR-Seq2P轻量级Seq2Point扩展模型，用于工业非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）的多任务分解，通过膨胀残差块和squeeze-and-excitation注意力在降低推理延迟的同时提升精度。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛机器学习/泛信号处理领域，核心任务是工业非侵入式负荷监测（NILM），即从聚合电力信号中分解出各机器负荷，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能均无直接关系。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感视觉任务。虽然论文涉及"lightweight"和"inference latency"等轻量化与部署概念，但其应用场景是工业电力负荷分解，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向弱相关，但其轻量级网络结构设计（dilated residual blocks + SE attention）和精度-延迟权衡评估思路对轻量化方法设计有一定借鉴意义。若时间有限可仅阅读其网络结构设计部分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）中的多任务负荷分解（multi-task disaggregation），即从聚合电力信号中估计多个工业机器负荷。应用场景：工业电力监测与能耗管理。传感器类型：电力/功率信号（非遥感影像）。目标类别：工业机器负荷（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: SEDR-Seq2P：基于Seq2Point架构的轻量级扩展，包含dilated residual blocks（膨胀残差块）和squeeze-and-excitation attention（SE注意力机制）。对比基线包括Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet。

Lightweight method: 轻量化主要体现在网络结构设计层面：采用膨胀残差块（dilated residual blocks）和squeeze-and-excitation attention（SE注意力）构建轻量级Seq2Point扩展，在保持精度的同时降低计算开销。摘要未明确提及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）或NAS等经典模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到SEDR-Seq2P相比WaveNet将推理延迟（inference latency）降低约58%，并强调"favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment"。但摘要未说明具体参数量（parameters）、FLOPs、显存占用、能耗等部署量化指标，也未涉及星上部署（onboard deployment）、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘硬件加速。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：IMDELD（工业多设备电力负荷数据集）。评价指标：能量估计指标（energy-estimation metrics，包括MAE、决定系数R²、匹配率match rate）以及accuracy-delay criterion（精度-延迟准则）。

Experiments: 在IMDELD上统一评估协议下对比Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet。结果显示：Seq2Point在精度-延迟平衡上优于Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq；GRU和WaveNet精度更高但计算成本显著更高。SEDR-Seq2P相对Seq2Point基线：MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提升约0.8%；相对WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：具体参数量与FLOPs、硬件平台与部署环境、模型在真实工业场景的鲁棒性、与其他轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）的对比。此外，该工作面向电力负荷信号而非遥感影像，其方法迁移到遥感目标检测场景需要重新验证。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但以下思路可借鉴：(1) dilated residual blocks（膨胀残差块）可在不增加参数量的情况下扩大感受野，对遥感小目标检测中提升多尺度特征提取能力有参考价值；(2) squeeze-and-excitation attention（SE注意力）作为轻量级通道注意力模块，计算开销小，适合嵌入遥感在轨检测骨干网络；(3) accuracy-delay trade-off（精度-延迟权衡）的评价思路与在轨部署中"精度-实时性"权衡的评估框架一致，可借鉴其评估方法论；(4) 轻量级网络设计（而非仅依赖后训练压缩）的思路值得参考，即在网络结构层面直接设计轻量化模型。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [88] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于光学显微图像的计算机视觉框架CV，用于无需物理测试即可估计轻质合金钢的疲劳寿命(log Nf)，包含OpenCV预处理、28维物理信息特征提取和CNN回归模型，并采用GNLL损失联合预测寿命与不确定性。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。论文聚焦于材料科学中的疲劳寿命预测，属于工业质量检测/材料表征领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。虽然使用了CNN回归和计算机视觉技术，但目标场景是光学显微图像下的钢材微观结构分析，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化没有直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为材料疲劳寿命预测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。仅当对不确定性估计或物理信息特征提取的通用方法论感兴趣时，可作为泛视觉方法参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：疲劳寿命回归预测（估计log Nf）。应用场景：轻质合金钢的质量控制/快速检测。传感器类型：光学显微镜（optical micrograph）。目标类别：钢材微观结构特征（裂纹形态、晶粒结构、孔隙率、纹理）。

Model architecture: 七阶段OpenCV预处理流程；28维物理信息特征提取器；CNN回归模型（评估了SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16三种架构）；采用Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL)损失函数联合预测log Nf和样本不确定性σ̂；使用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明明确的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提到运行时间"under 65 ms per image"，但未说明具体模型压缩或加速手段。三种评估架构中ResNet-50和VGG-16均为标准重型网络，未涉及轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到单张图像推理时间低于65 ms，但未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。该框架面向工业质检场景而非星上/边缘部署，与在轨遥感目标检测的实时推理、资源受限环境部署需求不匹配。摘要未说明是否适配FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘硬件。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成显微图像基准（synthetic micrograph benchmark）进行验证。评价指标：R²=0.93，RMSE=0.18 log-cycles，macro-F1=0.91；Expected Calibration Error (ECE)从0.089降至0.021（相对降低76%）。数据集生成器已开源。

Experiments: 在合成显微图像基准上对比了SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16三种架构，ResNet-50表现最佳（R²=0.93）。GNLL损失相比MSE基线将ECE降低76%。Grad-CAM验证网络关注冶金学上有意义的微观结构特征。但所有验证均基于合成数据，真实现场样本的域迁移研究是下一步工作。

Limitations: 1) 验证完全依赖合成显微图像，缺乏真实样本验证，域迁移问题未解决；2) 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；3) 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术；4) 未报告参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标；5) 任务本质是回归预测而非目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 尽管整体弱相关，仍有少量可借鉴点：1) GNLL损失用于联合预测目标值和不确定性的思路，可迁移到遥感小目标检测中输出检测置信度与不确定性估计；2) 物理信息特征提取（physics-informed features）的思想可启发遥感检测中融合领域先验知识（如地物光谱特性、几何约束）；3) Grad-CAM可解释性分析可用于验证遥感检测模型是否关注有物理意义的区域；4) 合成数据生成器+域迁移的验证范式，与遥感中合成数据（如仿真SAR图像）训练+真实数据微调的策略有相似性。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [89] [TerraNova: A Foundation Model for the Anthropocene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29527)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraNova 是一个地球系统基础模型（Earth-system foundation model），在原生几何（栅格地球系统场 + 国家行政指标）上联合建模物理地球与人类社会，通过跨模态 transformer 融合和超网络生成逐查询解码器，实现跨时空的国家级预测与稠密场重建。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文涉及 Earth-system、geospatial、遥感观测数据（gridded Earth-system fields），属于地球观测/地理空间建模范畴，但核心任务是"物理地球+人类社会耦合建模"的基础模型，并非遥感目标检测任务，也不涉及任何轻量化/在轨部署方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向仅在地理空间表示学习层面有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分。论文属于地球系统基础模型/地理空间表示学习方向，与遥感目标检测和轻量化部署均无直接关系，仅在多模态融合和快速适应范式上有间接借鉴价值，不建议作为核心阅读文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：地球系统基础模型（Earth-system foundation model），联合建模物理地球（栅格场）与人类社会（国家指标）。应用场景：地球观测、气候/社会耦合建模、国家级预测、稠密场重建。传感器类型：摘要未明确说明具体传感器，涉及 512 个栅格地球系统场（可能包含遥感/再分析数据）与 512 个国家指标。目标类别：非目标检测任务，无具体目标类别。

Model architecture: TerraNova 采用多编码器架构：1) 专用编码器分别表示 location、country、time、task 四类信息；2) cross-modal transformers 将多模态信息融合为共享时空状态（shared spatiotemporal state）；3) hypernetwork 生成 per-query decoder；4) evidential head 输出预测分布。两个对比学习目标：population-weighted alignment（国家与其领土内坐标对齐）和与预训练地理空间嵌入（image-derived semantics）的对齐。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS 等模型压缩或加速方法。仅提到"frozen backbone 可在消费级硬件上数分钟内适应未见变量"，暗示推理/微调开销较低，但未给出具体轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及 frozen backbone 可在 consumer hardware 上数分钟内适应 unseen variables，说明其微调成本较低，但未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 训练数据：1,024 个物理与社会记录，包括 512 个 gridded Earth-system fields 和 512 个 national indicators。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标，仅称其"competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders"，并支持 time、oceans、uncertainty 等额外维度。

Experiments: 摘要未给出详细实验对比。主要结论：1) 表示能力与专用地理空间编码器相当；2) 覆盖了专用编码器未表示的维度（时间、海洋、不确定性）；3) 支持国家级能力；4) frozen backbone 可从稀疏观测重建稠密场，并能在消费级硬件上数分钟内适应未见变量。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何检测任务；2) 未提供轻量化/部署相关技术细节；3) 摘要未说明具体传感器类型、数据来源和评价指标；4) 未给出模型规模、计算开销等关键工程指标；5) 属于基础模型/表示学习范式，与在轨实时推理场景差异较大。

Ideas for my research: 1) 其"多模态跨域融合 + 超网络生成逐查询解码器"的架构思想，可借鉴用于遥感多传感器（光学/SAR/高光谱）目标检测的特征融合设计；2) "frozen backbone + 快速适应未见变量"的范式，与在轨模型需要快速适应新场景/新目标类别的需求有相似性，可思考轻量微调/适配器（adapter）在星上部署中的应用；3) 对比学习对齐（population-weighted alignment）思想可迁移到遥感目标检测中的域对齐/跨传感器对齐；4) 但其整体范式（基础模型、国家级预测）与在轨目标检测的实时性、算力约束差异巨大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: A defining problem of the Anthropocene is to model the physical Earth and human societies as one coupled system, yet no learned representation spans their observational breadth. We argue the obstacle is geometric: the physical Earth is measured as continuous fields that ignore political borders, whereas societies are reported for administrative units. Earth-system foundation models serve the first geometry; coupling it to the second has required lossy averaging over borders. We introduce TerraNova, a foundation model trained on 1,024 physical and societal records in their native geometries: 512 gridded Earth-system fields and 512 national indicators. Dedicated encoders represent location, country, time and task, cross-modal transformers fuse them into a shared spatiotemporal state, and a hypernetwork generates a per-query decoder whose evidential head returns a predictive distribution. Two contrastive objectives couple the representation: a population-weighted alignment between each country and coordinates in its territory, and one to pretrained geospatial embeddings carrying image-derived semantics. Read out through that decoder, the representation is competitive with purpose-built geospatial encoders while spanning axes they do not represent (time, oceans and uncertainty) and supporting country-level capabilities. The frozen backbone reconstructs dense fields from sparse observations and adapts to unseen variables in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [90] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CALCoDe，一种面向冻结医学视觉语言模型的后处理可靠性层，通过类别尾部自适应与局部化共形预测结合，解决临床分布偏移下个别疾病类别覆盖不足的问题。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于医学视觉语言模型（medical VLM）与共形预测（conformal prediction）方向，核心是分布偏移下的类别覆盖可靠性，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关联。其方法本质是后处理可靠性校准层，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于医学VLM的共形预测可靠性研究。仅"类别级覆盖校准"和"post-hoc低开销校准层"两个概念有微弱的方法论借鉴价值，但整体优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学视觉语言模型的类别级覆盖可靠性校准（conformal prediction for medical VLMs）。应用场景：皮肤病学诊断（dermatology），涉及HAM10000、ISIC 2019、PAD-UFES-20三个皮肤病变数据集。传感器类型：非遥感，为医学皮肤影像。目标类别：皮肤疾病类别（具体类别名称摘要未说明）。

Model architecture: CALCoDe作为post-hoc reliability layer，叠加在冻结的医学VLM之上。核心组件包括：(1) cross-fitted validation predictions用于识别存在undercoverage风险的类别；(2) disjoint calibration split估计类别条件尾部阈值（class-conditional tail thresholds）；(3) 将protected threshold与localized conformal threshold通过one-sided maximum结合；(4) 独立的support audit用于defer（延迟决策）inlier support不足的样本。底层VLM backbone包括BiomedCLIP、OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32、MedSigLIP-448。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。其"post-hoc"性质仅指在冻结模型之上添加校准层，不改变模型本身的计算开销。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。其方法为后处理校准层，理论上计算开销较小（仅涉及阈值计算与集合构造），但论文未给出任何星上/边缘部署相关讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：HAM10000、ISIC 2019、PAD-UFES-20（皮肤病学数据集）。评价指标：observed marginal coverage、worst-class accepted coverage（目标值0.95）、average worst-class accepted coverage。对比方法：标准conformal baselines、sTACP、LCP-VLM等VLM-specific conformal方法。实验设置：两个分布偏移场景（HAM10000→ISIC 2019、HAM10000→PAD-UFES-20），四个冻结VLM backbone。

Experiments: 在全部8个设置（2个偏移场景×4个backbone）中，CALCoDe是唯一使observed marginal和worst-class accepted coverage均达到0.95的方法。在HAM10000→ISIC 2019上，其average worst-class accepted coverage为0.970，优于sTACP（0.926）和LCP-VLM（0.864）。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，属于医学影像与共形预测领域；(2) 方法依赖exchangeability假设（在受保护类别内），实际遥感场景中该假设可能不成立；(3) 摘要未说明方法在非医学VLM（如遥感基础模型）上的泛化性；(4) 未报告计算开销与部署指标；(5) 仅验证了皮肤病学两个偏移场景，泛化范围有限。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但以下思路可迁移到遥感在轨目标检测：(1) 类别级覆盖可靠性校准思想——遥感目标检测中不同目标类别（如舰船、飞机、车辆）在分布偏移（如不同传感器、不同光照、不同季节）下可能出现类别级性能退化，可借鉴"识别高风险类别+类别条件阈值"的思路做检测置信度校准；(2) post-hoc轻量校准层——在冻结的轻量化检测模型之上添加低开销校准模块，不增加推理负担，契合在轨部署对计算资源敏感的需求；(3) 分布偏移评估框架——遥感在轨场景中训练域（地面数据）与部署域（星上实时数据）存在分布差异，可借鉴其外部cohort评估思路设计跨域验证协议。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为核心方法（conformal prediction）与目标检测的anchor/NMS/框回归机制差异较大。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [91] [When Does On-Policy Interaction Help? Representational Tradeoffs in Value-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29617)
*Luca Viano,Antoine Moulin,Audrey Huang,Volkan Cevher,Philip Amortila,Dylan J. Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种交互式on-policy模仿学习算法OVI，证明专家交互可放宽学习者的表征需求（只需实现专家价值函数而非策略），并给出交互必要性的负结果。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛机器学习/强化学习领域的模仿学习（imitation learning）理论研究，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。唯一可能的间接联系是文中提到distillation（蒸馏）场景，但这是模仿学习语境下的概念，并非知识蒸馏模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于强化学习/模仿学习理论论文，仅在知识蒸馏的隐喻层面有极弱借鉴价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模仿学习（imitation learning）算法设计；应用场景：机器人、语言模型训练等通用领域；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未涉及任何遥感或视觉检测场景。

Model architecture: 提出OVI算法，属于交互式on-policy模仿学习框架，核心思想是利用专家交互来放宽学习者的表征能力要求——只需能表示专家价值函数（value function）而非完整策略（policy）。算法依赖linear maximization oracle实现计算效率。摘要未给出具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文虽提及distillation一词，但指的是模仿学习中"学习者无法完美表征专家策略"的蒸馏场景，并非模型压缩领域的knowledge distillation。未涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标，且论文场景与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅在理论层面给出统计效率和计算效率的论证，并提到实验对比了BC（offline policy-based）、DAgger（interactive policy-based）和offline value-based IL方法。

Experiments: 实验表明OVI在多个对比方法中表现最优，尤其在"学习者网络表达能力远弱于专家"时增益最大。同时给出负结果：在没有超出expert-value realizability的更强假设下，任何offline IL算法都必须随专家策略类复杂度扩展，证明交互的必要性。

Limitations: 1. 论文为纯理论性模仿学习研究，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化完全脱节；2. 摘要未说明实验的具体环境、数据集规模、任务类型；3. 理论结果（交互必要性、表征放宽）的实践可迁移性在遥感/视觉任务中未经验证；4. 未涉及任何部署约束或资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 1. 论文中"学习者表达能力不足时交互可补偿"的思想，在知识蒸馏（KD）用于轻量化遥感检测模型时有一定隐喻价值——当学生网络远小于教师时，可考虑引入教师交互式反馈（类似on-policy distillation）而非仅offline拟合，可能提升小模型蒸馏效果；2. 但该思路需要大量额外实验验证，且与遥感场景无直接映射，借鉴价值有限；3. 建议将注意力放在更直接相关的遥感检测轻量化文献上。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL)---training an agent to replicate expert behavior from demonstrations---underpins applications from robotics to language model training. Standard approaches such as Behavior Cloning (BC) are known to suffer from compounding errors and performance plateaus, particularly when the learner cannot perfectly represent the expert's policy (as is typical, e.g., in distillation). Two interventions are widely understood empirically to improve performance: querying the expert interactively along the learner's own trajectories, and using value function estimation en route to generating a policy rather than directly fitting the expert's full action distribution.
  We investigate the nature of these improvements and their potentially surprising interplay. Our main finding is that expert interaction relaxes the representational demands on the learner: one only needs a model capable of realizing the expert's value function, bypassing the (often stricter) requirement of realizing the expert's policy itself. Concretely, we introduce OVI, an interactive on-policy IL algorithm that is statistically efficient whenever the learner can represent the expert's value function and computationally efficient given access to a linear maximization oracle. We complement this with a negative result showing that interaction is necessary. Namely, without stronger assumptions beyond expert-value realizability alone, any offline IL algorithm must scale with the complexity of the expert policy class. Our findings bear out empirically. OVI outperforms offline policy-based (BC), interactive policy-based (DAgger), and offline value-based IL methods, with the largest gains when the learner network is substantially less expressive than the expert's.

</details>


### [92] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Missing-Data Flow Matching，将训练样本的缺失坐标视为潜变量并对 flow matching loss 取期望，理论上证明在完全随机缺失下该目标与完整数据目标等价，并给出有限样本分析与实验验证。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文聚焦于生成模型（flow matching）在缺失数据场景下的理论分析与方法改进，属于生成式机器学习/概率建模方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。既非遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于生成式机器学习理论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化部署基本无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感场景或任何模型压缩方法，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模（flow matching / generative modeling）在缺失数据（missing data）下的训练。应用场景：通用表格数据（tabular data）上的生成与插补（imputation）。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及目标检测类别。摘要未说明具体应用场景。

Model architecture: 基于 flow matching 框架，将缺失坐标作为潜变量（latent variables），对缺失值可能取值的分布上对 flow matching loss 取平均。核心是理论推导而非特定网络结构。摘要未说明具体网络架构（如是否使用 transformer、UNet 等 backbone）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理或边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该方法面向生成任务，与在轨目标检测的部署需求无关。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在真实表格数据（real tabular data）上进行，与经典插补方法（classical imputation）和深度插补基线（deep imputation baselines）对比。摘要未说明具体数据集名称、评价指标（如 FID、NLL 等）及实验设置细节。

Experiments: 数值实验验证了理论预测：1) 缺失性转移而非增加估计器方差；2) 每个样本一个补全（one completion）即可与完整数据方差精确匹配；3) 在固定评估预算下，一个补全是最优的；4) 确定性而非冻结的插补（deterministic rather than frozen imputation）才是导致生成分布坍缩的原因。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向基本无关，借鉴价值有限；2) 理论假设为完全随机缺失（MCAR）且存在真实补全，实际遥感数据缺失模式（如云遮挡、传感器故障）通常不满足该假设；3) 摘要未说明方法在图像/遥感影像上的泛化能力；4) 未涉及任何部署或效率考量。

Ideas for my research: 1) 尽管方向不同，其"将缺失坐标作为潜变量并对 loss 取期望"的思想，可类比迁移到遥感影像中云遮挡/条带噪声导致的像素缺失场景下的数据增强或自监督预训练；2) "确定性插补导致分布坍缩"的结论对遥感数据增强中避免模式坍缩有参考意义；3) 其关于"一个补全即最优"的结论提示在遥感数据增强中不必过度增加补全采样次数以节省计算。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [93] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMFGU，一个面向多模态联邦图学习的"遗忘"（unlearning）框架，通过目标特定表示解耦实现细粒度的多模态数据删除，同时保持保留内容的效用并防止信息通过剩余模态或图邻域被恢复。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于泛机器学习/图学习领域，涉及多模态联邦学习与数据遗忘（unlearning），与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。唯一可借鉴的点在于其"轻量级探针（lightweight probes）"和"紧凑原型与响应信号（compact prototype and response signals）"的设计思路，以及41.5×加速的压缩/加速思想，但这些与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）方法论完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题（多模态联邦图学习中的数据遗忘）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，既非遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏等轻量化方法。仅可作为泛机器学习方法略读，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态联邦图学习中的数据遗忘（unlearning）。应用场景：联邦学习环境下的用户数据删除请求（删除账户、删除图片/文本、撤销模态间关联）。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器，仅涉及结构化图数据、文本和视觉信号。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，处理对象是图节点/边/模态对应关系。

Model architecture: MMFGU框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 将异构遗忘请求映射为统一目标载体（unified target carriers）；(2) 目标特定表示解耦（target-specific representation decoupling），解耦待删除表示同时锚定保留语义；(3) 轻量级探针（lightweight probes）暴露并修复传播后的残差；(4) 通过紧凑原型和响应信号（compact prototype and response signals）选择性清除受影响的客户端。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"轻量化"元素有限：使用"轻量级探针（lightweight probes）"和"紧凑原型与响应信号（compact prototype and response signals）"来降低通信和计算开销，并实现了41.5×的加速（相对完整重训练）。但这属于联邦学习场景下的通信/计算效率优化，并非模型压缩（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）意义上的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存或能耗等部署指标。唯一的效率指标是相对完整重训练的41.5×加速比，但这是在联邦学习数据遗忘场景下的计算加速，与在轨推理部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及实验验证了"有效移除请求信息"、"保留图效用"以及"41.5×加速"。

Experiments: 摘要仅概述了实验结果：MMFGU能有效移除请求的信息、保留保留内容的图效用，并相对完整重训练实现41.5×加速。未给出具体对比方法、消融实验细节或数据集上的定量结果。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于联邦图学习与数据隐私领域；(2) 摘要未说明数据集、评价指标、模型规模等关键实验细节；(3) 未涉及任何模型压缩或推理加速方法；(4) 未讨论部署场景或硬件约束；(5) 多模态联邦图遗忘的设定与遥感在轨目标检测的工程需求差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 尽管整体弱相关，仍有少量可借鉴的抽象思路：(1) "目标特定表示解耦"的思想——在遥感目标检测轻量化中，可考虑对检测头中不同类别/尺度的特征进行解耦，仅对特定分支进行剪枝或量化，避免影响其他分支精度；(2) "轻量级探针"用于检测和修复残差——可类比为在轻量化模型中插入低开销的校准模块，用于检测量化/剪枝后引入的误差并局部修复；(3) "紧凑原型信号"降低通信开销——在联邦/分布式训练轻量化检测模型时，可借鉴用紧凑原型替代完整梯度/权重传输来降低通信成本。但这些均为间接启发，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [94] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"镜像学习"（mirror learning）框架，利用视频扩散模型和逆动力学模型，将第三人称被动观察转化为第一人称伪专家数据（mirror data），用于训练模仿学习策略。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于泛机器学习/机器人模仿学习领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。它关注的是从第三人称观察中学习动作策略的模仿学习问题，属于泛视觉/泛机器学习方法，对遥感在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本没有直接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于机器人模仿学习/生成式世界模型方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模仿学习（imitation learning）/行为克隆（behavior cloning）；应用场景：机器人策略学习、从第三人称演示中学习动作；传感器类型：视频观测（第一人称与第三人称视角）；目标类别：动作策略/控制轨迹，非视觉目标检测。

Model architecture: 框架由两部分组成：(i) 微调的视频扩散模型（fine-tuned video diffusion model）用于学习视角变换（perspective transformation），将第三人称观察转换为第一人称视角；(ii) 逆动力学模型（inverse dynamics model）用于在学习者控制空间中推断动作轨迹。二者组合生成"镜像数据"（mirror data），即由第三人称演示生成的伪第一人称专家数据。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法，也未讨论轻量化网络设计或边缘/星上部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。且视频扩散模型通常计算开销巨大，与在轨/边缘部署的轻量化需求相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及实验验证了"mirror data alone can train effective policies"以及"augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance"。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验细节。主要结论为：仅用镜像数据即可训练有效策略；将镜像数据与第一人称行为克隆训练结合可进一步提升下游策略性能。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：视频扩散模型推理成本高、实时性差；依赖逆动力学模型的准确性；视角变换可能存在信息损失；未涉及遥感/目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但存在两个潜在思路：(1) 视角变换/域适应思想——类似地，遥感多传感器（光学/SAR/多光谱）之间的域迁移或视角对齐可借鉴"视角变换"思路，用于跨传感器目标检测；(2) 数据增强思路——"mirror data"合成伪数据的思想可类比于遥感数据增强/仿真数据生成，用于扩充小样本遥感目标检测训练集。但整体上该论文与在轨轻量化目标检测方向关联度很低。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [95] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍TAGTorch——一个基于PyTorch的开源库，统一了受拓扑、代数和几何启发的深度学习工具，包括数据预处理、架构、训练技术和模型分析工具。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及具体数据集或评价指标。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。但可推断：该库面向拓扑/代数/几何结构数据，与遥感图像目标检测场景差异较大；且论文是软件工具介绍，缺乏针对遥感任务的验证。

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [96] [GQ-FSL: Green Quantized Federated Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29659)
*Idan Roth,Lutz Lampe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出绿色量化联邦分割学习框架GQ-FSL，通过随机量化和非对称精度配置，在满足精度约束下最小化无线边缘DNN部署的系统能耗。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文聚焦无线边缘设备上的联邦分割学习（FSL）与量化压缩，属于泛机器学习/边缘部署方法，与遥感、卫星、目标检测均无直接关联。虽然涉及量化（quantization）和边缘部署（edge deployment），但场景是移动设备与边缘服务器，并非遥感卫星在轨场景，也没有目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联，但量化与能耗建模方法对轻量化部署有一定方法论借鉴价值，可作为边缘部署量化技术的补充阅读，优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦分割学习（Federated Split Learning, FSL）下的DNN分布式训练与推理；应用场景：无线边缘计算（wireless edge），移动设备（mobile devices）与边缘服务器（edge server）协同；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 采用split learning架构，DNN在客户端（client-side submodel）和服务器端（server-side submodel）之间按cut layer切分；支持客户端与服务器端子模型采用非对称精度（asymmetric precision levels）；引入随机量化（stochastic quantization）用于本地协同训练和无线传输。

Lightweight method: 核心轻量化手段为量化（quantization）：对本地训练和无线传输均采用随机量化（stochastic quantization）；支持客户端与服务器端子模型使用不同精度（asymmetric precision），从而解耦设备能耗约束与全局收敛退化；通过联合优化DNN切分点（split point）和精度等级（precision levels）来最小化系统能耗。

Onboard deployability: 面向资源受限设备（resource-constrained devices）的部署，建立了参数化能耗模型（parameterized energy models）来量化split架构的能耗；提出联合优化问题在满足精度约束下最小化总系统能耗（EC）；相比量化联邦学习（quantized federated learning）和全精度FSL（full-precision FSL）具有更优的能效（energy efficiency）。但摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗数值，也未涉及星上/FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署细节。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集；评价指标包括系统能耗（energy consumption, EC）、目标精度（target accuracy）、理论收敛界（theoretical convergence bound）；实验设置摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要未给出具体实验对比结果，仅声称GQ-FSL相比quantized federated learning和full-precision FSL在能效上更优；提供了理论收敛界推导和联合优化问题求解，但具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 场景为无线边缘移动设备，非遥感卫星在轨场景，未涉及遥感数据、目标检测任务或星上硬件约束；2. 摘要未说明具体数据集、模型架构、精度数值、能耗节省比例等定量结果；3. 未涉及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等其他轻量化手段；4. 联邦学习场景与单星在轨推理场景差异较大，可迁移性有限。

Ideas for my research: 1. 非对称精度（asymmetric precision）思想可借鉴：在星上端（计算受限）与地面端（计算充裕）之间采用不同量化精度，解耦星上能耗约束与模型精度退化；2. 随机量化（stochastic quantization）用于训练和传输的思路可迁移到星上模型量化训练；3. 参数化能耗模型与联合优化（切分点+精度）的框架，可类比为星上推理时对模型切分/量化策略的联合优化；4. 理论收敛界推导方法可作为量化后模型精度保证的分析工具。

Abstract: Deploying state-of-the-art deep neural networks (DNNs) at the wireless edge is severely bottlenecked by the strict energy and resource constraints of mobile devices. While federated split learning (FSL) mitigates on-device computation by offloading workloads to an edge server, this may introduce systemic overheads, while the continuous exchange of cut-layer data, and submodels still incurs significant energy consumption (EC). To address this, we propose a green quantized FSL (GQ-FSL) framework that incorporates stochastic quantization for both local collaborative training and wireless transmissions. Notably, GQ-FSL supports asymmetric precision levels for the client- and server-side submodels, effectively decoupling device energy constraints from global convergence degradation. To quantify these tradeoffs, we develop parameterized energy models for the split architecture and derive a theoretical convergence bound under statistically heterogeneous data. Building on that, we formulate a joint optimization problem to configure the DNN split point and precision levels, minimizing the total system EC while satisfying a strict target accuracy constraint. Ultimately, we demonstrate that GQ-FSL enables large-scale DNN deployment on resource-constrained devices, achieving superior energy efficiency compared to quantized federated learning and full-precision FSL.

</details>


### [97] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将因子分解机（Factorization Machines）的特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINN）和神经算子学习中，用于增强参数化偏微分方程（PDE）解流形的模型表达能力。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文聚焦于科学计算/物理信息机器学习领域，研究参数化PDE求解，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能均无直接关联。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解。虽然涉及神经网络架构设计（特征交互模块），但属于泛机器学习/科学计算方法，对轻量化目标检测的借鉴意义极为有限。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于科学计算/物理信息机器学习领域，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：参数化偏微分方程（PDE）求解与神经算子学习（neural operator learning）。应用场景：科学计算、物理信息建模（physics-informed modeling），非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 提出三种模型：FM-PINN（将因子分解机特征交互嵌入PINN）、FM-Operator、FM-DeepONet。核心思想是利用多元函数二阶泰勒展开来刻画变量耦合，显式建模时空变量交互，并将空间坐标、时间、物理参数、初始/边界条件分组建模跨组交互。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该工作面向科学计算而非在轨遥感推理，不具备星上部署可行性讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。实验涉及平滑高阶PDE、非线性守恒律（nonlinear conservation laws）、具有尖锐梯度或间断（sharp gradients/discontinuities）的激波主导方程（shock-dominated equations）。评价指标为近似精度（approximation accuracy），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 数值实验表明：FM-PINN在平滑高阶PDE上提升近似精度；FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet在非线性守恒律和具有尖锐梯度/间断的问题上效果显著，但在平滑算子学习基准上无一致优势。未提供与目标检测或遥感相关实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于科学计算/物理信息机器学习范畴。2. 在平滑算子学习基准上无一致优势，泛化能力有限。3. 摘要未说明模型复杂度、计算开销、可扩展性等工程部署信息。4. 特征交互模块的计算成本可能较高，但摘要未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限，但可考虑以下泛化思路：1. 因子分解机式的特征交互建模思想（二阶交叉特征）在理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测中，用于建模多光谱/高光谱波段间的特征耦合关系，但需要重新设计为轻量模块。2. 跨组特征交互（如将空间坐标、时间、物理参数分组建模）的思路，可类比于遥感中"空间-光谱-时间"多模态特征融合，但需注意计算开销。总体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [98] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIPs，如MACE）的原子平均特征，用于无机晶体结构生成模型的评估与引导，并引入Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD)距离度量来同时捕捉生成结构的质量与新颖性。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于材料科学中的无机晶体结构生成与评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署等任何相关主题。属于生成式机器学习在材料科学领域的应用。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为材料科学中的晶体结构生成与评估，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无机晶体结构生成与生成模型评估；应用场景：材料科学/计算材料学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：无机晶体结构。

Model architecture: 使用预训练的Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs)，如MACE，提取原子平均特征（atom-averaged features）；引入Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD)作为评估度量框架，包含coarse和fine两个featurizer。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集；评价指标包括提出的CFTD（Coarse-Fine Transport Distance）以及对比的continuous SUN metrics。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验细节。主要展示了CFTD在捕捉晶体结构质量和检测记忆化（memorization）方面的能力，并与continuous SUN metrics进行了比较；同时展示了coarse MACE特征可作为材料生成模型的引导信号。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但显然该工作与遥感目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，其方法（晶体结构生成评估、MLIP特征）难以迁移到遥感在轨目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能间接相关的点是：论文使用预训练模型（MACE）提取特征并用于下游任务引导，这一"预训练特征+下游引导"的思路在概念上与遥感领域使用预训练backbone（如ImageNet预训练）类似，但具体方法（原子级特征、晶体结构距离度量）无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测或轻量化任务。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [99] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究利用网络安全预训练的8B参数大语言模型（LLM）通过prompt engineering提升自主网络防御决策，并提出在线policy distillation框架将LLM策略蒸馏到仅64,910参数的轻量RL智能体中，在CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境中验证了模型压缩与部署可行性。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向弱相关（2分）。论文属于网络安全/自主网络防御（Autonomous Cyber Operations）领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。但其核心方法——将大模型策略通过knowledge distillation蒸馏到轻量级agent中实现模型压缩与部署——在方法论层面（policy distillation、模型轻量化、边缘部署）对遥感在轨目标检测模型的轻量化有间接借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分（低优先级）。论文属于网络安全领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联，不涉及目标检测任务和遥感场景。仅在knowledge distillation方法论层面有间接借鉴价值，可作为轻量化方法综述性阅读的补充，不建议作为核心参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自主网络防御决策（Autonomous Cyber Operations, ACO），属于强化学习（Reinforcement Learning）决策任务，非目标检测任务。应用场景：企业网络防御（enterprise network defense），非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型：不适用（网络环境数据）。目标类别：网络攻击/防御行为，非遥感目标。环境：CybORG CAGE Challenge 2，网络规模4-12台主机。

Model architecture: 教师模型：8B参数、基于网络安全数据预训练的LLM（具体架构摘要未说明），通过prompt engineering（非fine-tuning）进行决策。学生模型：轻量RL agent，仅64,910参数。采用online policy distillation框架将LLM策略迁移到轻量RL agent中。

Lightweight method: 核心轻量化方法为knowledge distillation（知识蒸馏），具体采用online policy distillation框架，将8B参数的LLM教师策略蒸馏到64,910参数的轻量RL学生agent中，模型规模缩减数个数量级（several orders of magnitude）。摘要未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、NAS或TensorRT/FPGA等硬件加速手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及"lightweight, deployable agents"和"operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents"，强调模型压缩后可用于实际部署。但摘要未说明具体部署硬件平台（FPGA/NPU/边缘设备）、推理延迟（latency）、FLOPs、显存占用、能耗等量化部署指标。仅给出参数量对比：8B → 64,910参数（约缩减5个数量级）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集/环境：CybORG CAGE Challenge 2环境，构造了4到12台主机的网络配置场景以评估可迁移性。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标名称，但涉及防御能力（defensive capabilities）和策略对齐（policy-alignment）评估。实验设置：对比baseline RL agent、LLM教师策略、蒸馏后的轻量agent，以及teacher-guided RL stabilization策略。

Experiments: 主要结果：1) 8B参数LLM通过prompt engineering即可在CybORG CAGE Challenge 2中超越baseline RL agent；2) 蒸馏后的64,910参数轻量agent在保持有效防御能力的同时大幅缩减模型规模；3) 在4-12主机不同网络配置下评估了方法的可迁移性；4) teacher-guided RL stabilization策略均未一致超越优化后的教师策略，说明reward-driven RL优化与teacher-guided防御策略之间存在policy-alignment限制。

Limitations: 1) 场景为网络防御而非遥感/目标检测，任务类型差异大；2) 摘要未说明蒸馏后轻量agent相对教师模型的具体性能损失（accuracy drop）数值；3) 摘要未提供推理延迟、FLOPs、能耗等部署关键指标；4) 未涉及遥感图像特征（空间分辨率、多光谱通道、旋转目标等）；5) 未讨论在计算资源受限的星上环境（onboard）中的可行性；6) teacher-guided stabilization策略的policy-alignment问题可能同样存在于遥感检测蒸馏中，需注意。

Ideas for my research: 1) **大模型蒸馏到轻量检测器的思路可借鉴**：虽然本文是LLM→RL agent的policy distillation，但其"大模型作为教师、轻量模型作为学生"的范式可类比到遥感目标检测中"大检测模型（如DINO、YOLO大模型）→轻量检测器"的knowledge distillation；2) **online distillation框架**：本文采用在线蒸馏而非离线蒸馏，遥感在轨检测中可考虑在线蒸馏以适配动态数据分布；3) **prompt engineering替代fine-tuning**：在遥感场景中，可探索用prompt engineering引导大模型生成伪标签或辅助监督信号，避免大模型微调的高成本；4) **参数量压缩数量级参考**：8B→64K（约5个数量级）的压缩幅度为遥感在轨检测模型的压缩目标提供了参考；5) **policy-alignment问题警示**：教师策略与RL优化目标不一致的问题，在遥感检测蒸馏中对应"教师模型与轻量学生模型在特征分布上的对齐困难"，需设计合适的蒸馏损失。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments. However, RL agents typically require extensive exploration during training, often resulting in unstable behavior and poor initial decision-making before converging toward effective defense strategies.
  In this work, we investigate the use of a Large Language Model (LLM) to improve autonomous defensive decision-making within an ACO environment. Through prompt engineering rather than fine-tuning, we demonstrate that an 8-billion parameter LLM pretrained on cybersecurity data can outperform a baseline RL agent in a modified CybORG CAGE Challenge 2 environment. We then propose an online policy distillation framework that transfers the LLM's defensive policy into a lightweight RL agent containing only 64,910 parameters, reducing model size by several orders of magnitude while maintaining effective defensive capabilities. This provides a pathway toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within lightweight, deployable agents.
  To evaluate transferability, we construct CybORG scenarios ranging from 4 to 12 hosts and assess the approach across varying network configurations. We also evaluate teacher-guided RL stabilization strategies and observe that none consistently surpass the optimized teacher policy, suggesting policy-alignment limitations between reward-driven RL optimization and teacher-guided defense strategies.
  Our results demonstrate the potential of cybersecurity-focused LLMs as sources of expertise for autonomous cyber defense, while policy distillation provides a practical path toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents.

</details>


### [100] [Hypergradient-based Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Improved Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28849)
*Naman Saxena,Mudit Gaur,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Boltzmann策略最优性的无Hessian超梯度双层强化学习算法，在温和正则条件下获得O(ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度和最优样本复杂度，并去除了外层目标函数的PL条件假设。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文属于强化学习理论算法研究，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及object detection、目标检测、轻量化、pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、边缘部署等任何与在轨遥感目标检测及模型轻量化相关的内容。属于泛机器学习/强化学习理论方法，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文为强化学习理论算法研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非课题涉及bilevel RL在检测模型优化中的应用，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：bilevel reinforcement learning（双层强化学习）算法设计与收敛性分析。应用场景：meta-learning、hierarchical task decomposition、RL-HF等通用RL问题。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 提出hypergradient-based bilevel RL算法，利用entropy regularized discounted RL目标函数下Boltzmann policy的最优性来构造超梯度，算法为Hessian-free设计。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要中没有任何关于模型压缩、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘/星上部署的内容。虽然算法是Hessian-free（避免二阶Hessian计算），但这属于算法计算效率优化，而非模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该工作聚焦理论收敛性分析（iteration complexity和sample complexity），不涉及实际部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为iteration complexity O(ε⁻¹)和sample complexity Õ(ε⁻²)，属于理论复杂度分析指标，而非目标检测任务中的mAP、FPS等指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明实验设置、对比方法或消融实验。该工作主要为理论贡献，包括：1) 提出Hessian-free的hypergradient-based bilevel RL算法；2) 获得O(ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度和Õ(ε⁻²)样本复杂度；3) 去除了先前工作中外层目标函数的Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件假设。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可指出：该工作为纯理论算法研究，未涉及任何遥感或目标检测应用场景，也未涉及模型轻量化或部署问题。其理论结果（如Hessian-free设计、去除PL条件）虽在RL理论上有价值，但与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联性很弱。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) Hessian-free的超梯度计算思想在理论上可降低计算开销，若未来在遥感目标检测中引入bilevel优化框架（如meta-learning用于跨传感器域适应、或RL-HF用于检测模型调优），可参考其避免二阶计算的设计思路；2) 去除PL条件假设的收敛性分析框架，对设计遥感检测模型训练算法的理论保证有一定参考价值。但整体而言，该论文与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) is an important framework within the literature of RL that can be used to formalize various categories of problems, such as meta-learning, hierarchical task decomposition, and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RL-HF). Most of the bilevel RL algorithms are either not scalable because of using hypergradient with Hessian, or they suffer from high sample complexity because of using penalty-based approximation methods. In this work, we propose a hypergradient-based bilevel RL algorithm using the optimality of the Boltzmann policy for the entropy regularized discounted RL objective function. Our proposed algorithm is Hessian-free and obtains an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-1})$ and state-of-the-art sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ under mild regularity conditions. Further, in our convergence analysis, we are able to remove the assumption of the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition on the outer-level objective function present in the prior state-of-the-art sample complexity work.

</details>


### [101] [An analysis of machine learning approaches for enhancing decision-making in complex discrete choice tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28854)
*Sheng Lun Christine Cao,Destenie Nock,Alex Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估四种机器学习模型（多项逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络、高斯过程）在离散选择建模中学习个体选择规则的能力，并通过蒙特卡洛实验和能源政策偏好数据案例验证半参数与非参数模型的优势。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是离散选择建模（discrete choice modeling）与偏好诱导（preference elicitation），属于行为科学、政策制定和能源政策领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型压缩或部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于行为科学/政策分析领域的离散选择建模研究，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：离散选择建模与个体偏好学习（discrete choice modeling / preference elicitation）。应用场景：政策制定（policy-making）、能源政策偏好分析。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感/视觉任务）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 评估了四种模型：multinomial logistic regression（参数模型）、generalized additive model（半参数模型）、twinned neural network (TNN)（非参数模型）、Gaussian process（非参数模型）。用于学习五种选择规则：linear strong utility、monotonic strong utility、ideal point、lexicographic semiorder、multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法，如剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS等均未提及。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。本文关注的是模型预测精度（BIC指标）而非计算效率。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：蒙特卡洛模拟实验数据（改变属性数量、训练选择集数量、选择规则确定性三个维度）+ 真实能源政策偏好数据（case study）。评价指标：BIC（Bayesian Information Criterion），以及模型性能随训练选择集和确定性提升的百分比改善（6%-96%和0%-55%）。

Experiments: 蒙特卡洛实验表明：半参数和非参数模型（GAM、TNN、GP）在所有选择规则和实验情境下普遍优于参数模型（multinomial logistic regression）。增加训练选择集和选择规则确定性分别带来6%-96%和0%-55%的性能提升。真实能源政策数据案例中，TNN表现最佳，BIC为13.351。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性，但可推断：1）研究聚焦于行为科学/政策领域的选择规则，与视觉/遥感任务无关；2）未涉及任何计算效率、模型规模或部署可行性分析；3）实验基于模拟数据和单一真实案例，泛化性有限。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：论文强调"任务上下文应驱动模型选择"（choice task context should drive model selection），这一方法论思想可类比为"部署场景（星上算力约束、实时性要求）应驱动轻量化策略选择"，但这是非常泛化的方法论启示，不构成实质性借鉴。

Abstract: Discrete choice modeling is a common tool used for preference elicitation during policy-making, but this is typically done through parametric models. Machine learning can push the boundaries of discrete choice modeling for policy-based preference elicitation by adopting a data-driven approach or learning individual preferences. However, there is limited knowledge of how well machine learning methods can estimate individual discrete choice rules under individual heterogeneity, especially in the context of challenges often experienced during preference elicitation. This study evaluates four machine learning models (multinomial logistic regression, generalized additive model, twinned neural network, and Gaussian process) with respect to their capacity to learn and predict five choice rules that are important in the behavioral and social sciences (linear strong utility, monotonic strong utility, ideal point, lexicographic semiorder, and multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator). Monte Carlo experiments were performed to assess model performance when increasing a) the number of attributes in the choice alternatives, b) the number of training choice sets, and c) the choice rule's determinism. The simulation results demonstrated that semi-parametric and non-parametric models generally outperform parametric models across all choice rules and experimental contexts. Model performance also generally improves by 6% to 96% and 0% to 55%, respectively, with an increase in training choice sets and choice rule determinism. A case study using real energy policy preference data was also conducted, where TNN performed best with a BIC of 13.351. This work demonstrated the viability and limitations of semi-parametric and non-parametric models in the context of policy-centric discrete choice modeling and showed how the choice task context should drive model selection.

</details>


### [102] [Fast Rates for Swap-Agnostic Learning of Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28856)
*Princewill Okoroafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"swap-agnostic learning"（交换无关学习）框架，通过将预测级比较转化为二阶多校准问题，为任意固定proper loss提供了离线与在线的学习算法，并给出了紧致的样本复杂度和regret界。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于纯理论机器学习（learning theory）范畴，研究swap-agnostic learning、proper loss、multicalibration、Blackwell approachability等概念，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何直接或间接关联。弱相关原因：既非遥感场景，也非目标检测任务，更不涉及任何模型压缩或部署方法，属于泛机器学习理论工作。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于纯学习理论论文，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
