Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [198] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMTFV（Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification），通过数学工具流接口将验证建模与具体执行解耦，利用数学工具箱实现精确计算，提升大语言模型数学问题求解的答案验证能力。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型（LLM）的数学推理答案验证问题，属于自然语言处理/数学推理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或任何模型压缩/加速技术。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为LLM数学推理验证，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型数学推理答案验证（mathematical reasoning verification）。应用场景：LLM数学问题求解的候选答案校验与修正。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感/视觉任务）。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AMTFV采用agentic架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) 验证agent（verification agent），负责构建验证工作流、编码数学对象与计算意图为MTF请求；(2) 数学工具箱agent（mathematical toolbox agent），负责解析请求、生成可执行调用并分派到后端进行精确计算。引入Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF)作为interrupt-execute-resume接口，将验证建模与具体执行解耦。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法，也未涉及边缘部署或实时推理优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息，也未讨论星上或边缘部署可行性。其agentic多agent架构本身计算开销较大，与在轨轻量化部署需求相悖。

Datasets and metrics: 在5个具有挑战性的数学推理数据集上评估，使用7种模型配置（来自DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini）。评价指标为准确率（accuracy），报告了相对最强baseline平均准确率最高提升8.3个百分点，且在中高验证复杂度样本上增益更大。

Experiments: AMTFV在5个数学推理数据集、7种模型配置下整体优于所评估的代表性baseline方法。在单个模型配置下，相对最强baseline平均准确率提升最高达8.3个百分点，中高验证复杂度样本上提升更显著。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但可推断：(1) 论文完全面向LLM数学推理，与遥感/视觉/目标检测无关；(2) agentic多agent架构计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上部署场景；(3) 未报告推理延迟、能耗等部署指标；(4) 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 对本课题（遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化）的可借鉴点非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：其"将建模与执行解耦"的模块化设计思想，在某种程度上类似于将检测模型的"特征提取"与"检测头"解耦的模块化轻量化设计思路，但这一关联非常牵强。总体而言，该论文对本课题几乎没有直接可借鉴的技术或方法。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [199] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COntExt框架，利用结构化运营指标定义中的上下文信息来自动扩展本体论（ontology），将本体扩展问题分解为父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性分配三个子任务。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于知识工程/本体论（ontology）扩展领域，涉及网络安全本体和运营指标目录，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型压缩/加速等任何相关技术。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于知识工程/本体论扩展领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测和模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：本体论自动扩展（ontology extension）；应用场景：企业运营指标管理与知识工程；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未涉及任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: COntExt框架将本体扩展问题分解为三个子任务：parent class prediction（父类预测）、relation type prediction（关系类型预测）、data property assignment（数据属性分配）。摘要未说明具体使用的模型架构或算法细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法，如pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等均未提及。论文不涉及模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用四个网络安全本体（four cybersecurity ontologies）作为评估数据集。摘要未说明具体评价指标和实验设置细节。

Experiments: 在四个网络安全本体上评估了不同算法在各子任务上的表现。结果显示，基于指标上下文（metric-derived context）的建议在relation type prediction和data property assignment两个任务上优于基于本体上下文的基线方法。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显与遥感目标检测和轻量化方向完全无关，属于知识工程/语义网领域的研究。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴之处。唯一可能的间接启发是：论文强调利用"上下文信息"来提升预测效果，这一思想在目标检测中也有类似体现（如利用场景上下文辅助小目标检测），但关联非常间接，不构成实质性借鉴。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [200] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DungeonBench，一个用于《龙与地下城》战斗场景战术推理的基准测试平台，评估语言模型策略在复杂规则交互下的决策能力。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于游戏（Dungeons & Dragons）中的战术推理基准测试，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。属于泛机器学习/强化学习/语言模型评估方向，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于游戏AI/语言模型推理评估领域，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：游戏战术推理（tactical reasoning）基准测试；应用场景：Dungeons & Dragons（D&D）桌面角色扮演游戏战斗模拟；传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不适用（涉及游戏中的移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备、稀缺资源等决策选项）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文提到支持多种策略类型：heuristic controllers（启发式控制器）、language-model policies（语言模型策略）、learned option rankers（学习型选项排序器）、masked-action reinforcement-learning agents（掩码动作强化学习智能体）。评估对象为frontier language-model policies（前沿语言模型策略）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计或任何模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DungeonBench基准测试，包含两个track——Encounter（单场战斗的局部战术评估）和Day（通过持久生命值、法术位、消耗品、准备和短休时机串联多场遭遇战）。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，主要评估策略在直接遭遇战中的胜率以及跨遭遇战中的资源预算、休息时机和规则感知战术纪律表现。

Experiments: 实验评估了frontier language-model policies（前沿语言模型策略）在共享决策流上的表现。结果显示：完整战术观测并未使基准饱和——前沿策略在直接遭遇战中常能获胜，但在串联遭遇战（Day track）中暴露出资源预算（resource budgeting）、休息时机（rest timing）和规则感知战术纪律（rule-aware tactical discipline）方面的失败。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向完全无关；此外，摘要未提供模型参数量、计算开销、推理效率等任何与部署相关的信息。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的可借鉴点非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：论文强调"完整观测并不饱和基准"这一结论，提示在评估模型能力时，单纯增加输入信息量（如遥感图像分辨率/波段数）并不必然带来性能提升，需要关注模型在资源约束下的决策质量——这与在轨场景中"有限计算资源下如何权衡信息利用与推理效率"有一定类比意义，但属于非常间接的关联。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [201] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentHPOBench，一个用于评估LLM智能体在机器学习实验中顺序超参数优化能力的基准测试，包含30个可执行ML任务。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于LLM作为科学智能体的实验执行与超参数优化能力评估，属于机器学习/自动化ML（AutoML）领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于LLM智能体评估与AutoML领域，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM智能体顺序超参数优化（HPO）能力评估；应用场景：科学实验自动化、AutoML；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及12种广泛使用的LLM agents和传统HPO基线方法，但未给出具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文不讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计等模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AgentHPOBench，包含30个可执行机器学习任务，覆盖7个研究类别；评价指标：实验优化能力、迭代精炼能力、日志诊断能力、向参考性能的收敛一致性。

Experiments: 在统一协议下评估了12种广泛使用的agents和传统HPO基线。结果显示当前agents在跨领域表现出可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代精炼、复杂日志诊断和向参考性能稳定收敛方面仍存在明显局限。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关。摘要未涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关主题。该工作属于LLM智能体评估与AutoML领域，对遥感在轨检测轻量化课题无直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：该论文提出的"顺序干预-观察-再决策"的智能体评估框架，在方法论层面可类比于自动化超参数搜索（如NAS搜索轻量网络结构）的迭代优化思路。如果未来研究涉及用LLM辅助自动化设计轻量检测网络架构或自动调参，此框架的评估协议有一定参考意义，但属于间接借鉴。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [202] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FDD-ON本体，用于形式化表示VAV HVAC系统的故障检测与诊断语义，实现异构FDD数据的互操作与知识查询。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于建筑暖通空调（HVAC）系统的故障检测与诊断（FDD）本体建模，属于建筑能源管理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"fault detection"和"diagnosis"在字面上与检测相关，但这里的检测对象是HVAC设备故障，而非遥感图像中的目标。评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于建筑HVAC故障诊断领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：HVAC系统故障检测与诊断（FDD）语义建模；应用场景：建筑能源管理、数字孪生FDD框架、AI驱动的维护决策系统；传感器类型：VAV HVAC系统传感器数据；目标类别：HVAC设备故障类型、症状状态、故障影响。

Model architecture: FDD-ON本体架构，包含模块化可扩展的本体设计，形式化表示VAV HVAC系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及关联属性，并集成受控词汇表（controlled vocabulary）以及故障/症状/影响库。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法，如pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等均未提及。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用公开可用的VAV HVAC系统数据集进行评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行演示。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明FDD-ON在公开VAV HVAC数据集上进行了评估，并通过FDD开发应用进行了演示，结果表明其提供了可扩展、透明、可互操作的FDD解决方案的语义基础框架。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。主要局限在于：1）仅针对HVAC/VAV领域，领域知识不可迁移到遥感场景；2）本体建模方法不涉及任何视觉模型或深度学习模型；3）未涉及任何计算效率、部署或实时推理问题。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题几乎没有直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：本体/语义建模的思路可用于构建遥感目标检测的领域知识库或标注规范，但这与本课题的核心（轻量化模型设计）关联极弱。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [203] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ExtractBench，一个用于 schema-guided extraction（模式引导抽取）的基准测试，首次同时评估 value accuracy、record completeness、grounding 和 cost，并发布 LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 作为领先方法。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于企业文档的 schema-guided extraction（模式引导抽取），属于 NLP/文档理解/LLM agent 领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解。轻量化方面仅涉及"cost"（成本）的度量，但这是指 API 调用成本而非模型压缩/部署成本，与 pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等轻量化手段无关。相关性评分：1 分。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于企业文档抽取/NLP agent 领域，技术栈、任务、指标、部署场景均不匹配，无需深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：schema-guided extraction（模式引导抽取），即给定文档和用户定义 schema，agent 遵循 schema 生成正确输出并附带 source evidence 作为 grounding 元数据。应用场景：企业文档处理（370 份企业文档、8 个业务领域、67 种文档类型）。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：不适用（非视觉目标检测，而是文档中的结构化信息抽取）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构细节。涉及的方法包括：Commercial VLMs（商业视觉语言模型）、coding agents（编码智能体）、以及 LlamaExtract Agentic Plus。这些属于 LLM/VLM agent 类方法，与遥感目标检测网络（如 CNN/Transformer 检测器）无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及传统模型轻量化方法（pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS 等均未提及）。论文中的"cost"指标衡量的是 agent 运行/API 调用的经济成本，而非模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存或能耗等部署指标。摘要未说明任何模型压缩或加速手段。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文面向企业文档抽取的云端/API 服务场景，不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT 等。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息，仅提及"measured cost"（测量成本），指运行成本而非计算资源开销。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ExtractBench，包含 4,869 页、370 份企业文档、8 个业务领域、67 种文档类型，并带有区分挑战场景的标签。数据构建采用三通道：独立系统一致性（真实文档）、已知值（合成列表）、人工验证（表单）。评价指标：order-insensitive value F1（值准确率）、record completeness（记录完整性）、word-level 和 page-level F1（两种 grounding 溯源指标）、以及 measured cost（成本）。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：Commercial VLMs 在短文档上表现良好，但在长文档上经常截断记录列表；coding agents 保持更高准确率但成本显著更高；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在三个指标上均排名第一，准确率与 coding agents 相当但成本仅为一小部分。摘要未提供与遥感目标检测相关的对比实验或消融实验。

Limitations: 与本研究方向基本无关，主要局限在于：1) 面向企业文档抽取而非遥感图像理解；2) 不涉及任何视觉目标检测任务；3) 不涉及模型轻量化/压缩/部署技术；4) 评价体系（value F1、grounding F1、cost）与遥感检测指标（mAP、Recall、FLOPs、延迟）完全不同，无可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的可借鉴点非常有限。唯一可能间接相关的启发是：论文强调在保证准确率的同时控制"cost"，这与轻量化追求"精度-效率"平衡的理念在哲学层面相似，但技术路径完全不同。此外，其"独立系统一致性 + 已知值 + 人工验证"的多通道数据标注/验证思路，或许可类比借鉴到遥感小目标检测数据集的标注质量校验中，但属于非常间接的迁移。总体而言，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [204] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR)，一种无外部反馈的多轮强化学习框架，让语言模型通过自验证信号作为计算控制策略，实现自适应测试时计算分配，在数学推理基准上以更少的推理轮数超越强基线。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于大语言模型（LLM）推理与强化学习领域，核心是数学推理任务中的自适应测试时计算分配（adaptive test-time compute allocation）。它不涉及遥感、卫星、地球观测、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。唯一的间接关联在于"自适应计算分配"思想（即对简单输入减少计算、对困难输入增加计算）在概念上可类比于遥感在轨场景中根据图像复杂度动态调整推理开销，但论文本身与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于 LLM 数学推理与强化学习领域。仅"自适应测试时计算分配"这一高层思想有间接借鉴价值，但迁移难度大，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型数学推理（mathematical reasoning）与自适应测试时计算分配。应用场景：通用自然语言推理，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用（文本输入）。目标类别：数学问题求解，非视觉目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于 Qwen3.5-2B 语言模型，采用多轮强化学习框架。每轮模型同时输出：1) 一个解决方案（solution）；2) 离散的正确性判定（discrete correctness verdict）；3) 置信度分数（confidence score）。当判定为 Correct 且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则利用自身自验证继续细化（refinement）。训练使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）在固定水平轨迹上训练。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。但论文的核心思想是"自适应测试时计算分配"（adaptive test-time compute allocation），即通过自验证策略控制推理轮数，对简单输入减少计算、对困难输入增加计算，从而在推理阶段降低平均计算开销（平均仅 2.99 个推理轮次）。这属于推理时动态计算调度，而非模型参数层面的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。模型为 Qwen3.5-2B（约 20 亿参数），规模较大，不适合直接星上部署。摘要仅提及平均推理轮数 2.99 轮（相比固定 10 轮显著减少），体现了推理阶段计算量的降低，但未给出具体硬件部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：7 个数学推理基准（seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks），具体名称摘要未说明。评价指标：macro-average accuracy（宏平均准确率），达到 0.563；平均推理轮数（inference turns）2.99。对比方法：标准 GRPO、强多轮基线、固定预算的 oracle-guided score-feedback 参考方法。

Experiments: 在 7 个数学推理基准上，SVR 以平均 2.99 个推理轮次达到宏平均准确率 0.563。在完整系统对比中，SVR 超越标准 GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的 oracle-guided score-feedback 参考方法，同时所需推理轮数远少于固定 10 轮推理。摘要未提供逐基准详细结果和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，属于 LLM 推理领域；2) 摘要未说明具体数据集名称、逐基准结果、消融实验；3) 未提供计算开销的量化指标（FLOPs、延迟、能耗）；4) 模型规模（2B 参数）较大，不适用于资源受限的星上环境；5) 自验证机制依赖语言模型的文本生成能力，难以直接迁移到视觉目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 1) "自适应计算分配"思想可借鉴：在遥感在轨目标检测中，可设计基于图像复杂度/难度的动态推理调度策略——对简单场景（如均匀海面）使用轻量快速推理路径，对复杂场景（如密集港口、多目标）启用更深的推理分支，从而在平均意义上降低星上计算开销；2) "自验证/置信度阈值"机制可迁移：检测模型可输出置信度，当置信度低于阈值时触发二次细化推理（如更高分辨率特征或旋转框精修），类似 SVR 的 stop-ready 状态控制；3) 强化学习训练奖励设计思路（正确性+校准感知自验证）可用于训练检测模型的"何时停止/何时细化"策略，实现推理时自适应。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>

### [205] [Advances, challenges, and opportunities for legged robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28952)
*Jonas Frey,Matías Mattamala,Hae-Won Park,Mayank Mittal,Georg Martius,Maike Osborne,Robert Sparrow,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于人形机器人和四足机器人（legged robots）的综述性论文，评估其在硬件、运动、自主性、数据和应用方面的能力现状与未来展望。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文主题是腿式机器人（humanoid and quadrupedal robots），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。也不涉及目标检测、轻量化、模型压缩或边缘部署。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为腿式机器人综述，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：腿式机器人能力综述与展望。应用场景：机器人社会应用、科学发现、伦理与经济影响。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文聚焦于机器人硬件、locomotion（运动控制）、autonomy（自主性）等系统层面，而非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等任何模型压缩或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集，也未提及评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。这是一篇综述/展望性质论文，未报告实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。摘要聚焦于腿式机器人的社会影响、伦理、经济与政策层面，属于机器人学与社会科学交叉领域，对目标检测模型压缩没有任何直接或间接的借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化，无法为"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题提供任何可复现方向或改进思路。

Abstract: Humanoid and quadrupedal robots have the potential to revolutionize the way we work, interact, and coexist with intelligent machines. To understand their effects on society and how they can enable scientific discovery, we assess the current capabilities of these systems along hardware, locomotion, autonomy, data, and applications. We identify recent advances and key open challenges that must be overcome to enable widespread adoption and new use cases for legged robots. Last, we provide an outlook on the future of legged robots, exploring their ethical considerations, economic potential, policy implications, and broader societal effects.

</details>


### [206] [ST-WAM: Semantic-Temporal World Action Model for Robust Manipulation under Visual Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28993)
*Mingxin Wang,Bin Hu,Bin Qian,Kaitao Jiang,Haoning Wu,Feng Yan,Bowen Jing,Ruiyang Hao,Enyi Wang,Kangning Niu,Yandan Yang,Mu Xu,Yan Wang,Houde Liu,Tianlun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Semantic-Temporal WAM (ST-WAM)，利用 DINOv3 语义特征替代像素生成式未来预测，通过双空间未来专家（DSFE）和当前锚定意图检索（CAIR）提升机器人操作在视觉分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于机器人操作（robot manipulation）与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）建模领域，核心是 World Action Models 的未来动态预测与视觉分布偏移鲁棒性。它不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS 等轻量化方法。唯一可借鉴的是其利用 DINOv3 语义特征替代像素生成式预测的思路，以及"语义-时序建模"对分布偏移鲁棒性的提升，这与遥感在轨场景中光照/季节/视角变化下的特征鲁棒性有一定类比价值，但整体相关性较弱。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于机器人操作/VLA领域。仅其"语义特征替代像素生成"和"双空间建模"思想对遥感域鲁棒性设计有间接借鉴价值，不建议作为核心参考文献精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作（robot manipulation）中的动作预测与未来视觉动态建模（World Action Model）。应用场景：LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 等机器人操作基准，以及真实世界机器人操作。传感器类型：视觉相机（RGB）。目标类别：机器人操作任务中的物体与动作意图，非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: ST-WAM 采用双空间未来专家（Dual-Space Future Experts, DSFE）联合预测未来 VAE latents 和 DINOv3 特征；Current-Anchored Intent Retrieval (CAIR) 在当前视觉-语言上下文下从近期 DINO 历史中检索任务相关证据。整体为端到端训练，推理时无需显式未来生成。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。推理时"无需显式未来生成"可视为一种计算开销的降低，但并非传统意义上的轻量化手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署指标。虽然推理时省去了未来生成步骤，但 DINOv3 特征提取本身计算量较大，在星上/边缘部署可行性未讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO（含 LIBERO-Plus 零样本测试）、RoboTwin 2.0、真实世界机器人操作实验。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate），LIBERO 上 98.7%，RoboTwin 2.0 上 92.8%；零样本 LIBERO-Plus 相比 Fast-WAM 提升 21.3 个百分点；真实世界视觉偏移下成功率从 25.8% 提升至 61.5%。

Experiments: 主要对比对象为 Fast-WAM。实验验证了：1) 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上达到高成功率；2) 零样本 LIBERO-Plus 性能提升 21.3 个百分点；3) 真实世界视觉偏移下成功率从 25.8% 提升至 61.5%（超过两倍）。此外通过 frame-triplet 诊断实验证明 DINOv3 特征在视觉偏移下比 Wan-VAE latents 更稳定且更好保留任务状态区分。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于机器人操作领域；2) 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、推理延迟等部署指标；3) 未涉及任何轻量化技术；4) DINOv3 作为共享语义表示的计算开销未讨论；5) 未说明在极端视觉偏移（如遥感中的光照、云雾、季节变化）下的泛化能力。

Ideas for my research: 1) "语义特征替代像素生成式预测"的思想可类比到遥感目标检测中：在光照/季节/传感器差异等分布偏移下，用语义级特征（如 DINOv3 或自监督特征）替代像素级重建，可提升遥感场景下的域鲁棒性；2) "双空间联合建模"（粗粒度语义 + 细粒度动态）可借鉴为遥感检测中的"语义分支 + 细节分支"双流设计，兼顾鲁棒性与精度；3) "历史检索"（CAIR）思想可类比为遥感时序影像中的时序上下文利用，用于小目标检测的时序增强；4) 其"推理时无需显式生成"的省算力思路，可启发在轨检测中避免冗余计算的设计。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm by jointly modeling robot actions and future visual dynamics. However, their reliance on pixel-generative future supervision can entangle action-relevant state transitions with task-irrelevant visual content, limiting robustness under visual distribution shifts. We identify Training-Distribution Hallucination, a recurring phenomenon in which futures conditioned on visually shifted observations hallucinate training-domain content rather than remain faithful to the current scene. A controlled frame-triplet diagnosis further shows that DINOv3 features remain more stable across visual shifts while better preserving task-state distinctions than Wan-VAE latents. Rather than correcting the predicted futures, we propose Semantic-Temporal WAM (ST-WAM) to improve action robustness by using DINOv3 as a shared semantic representation for future prediction and history retrieval while retaining fine-grained VAE dynamics. Its Dual-Space Future Experts (DSFE) jointly predict future VAE latents and DINO features, while Current-Anchored Intent Retrieval (CAIR) retrieves task-relevant evidence from recent DINO history under the current visual-language context. ST-WAM is trained end-to-end without additional embodied pretraining or task-specific annotations, and requires no explicit future generation at inference. It achieves 98.7% on LIBERO and 92.8% on RoboTwin 2.0; more importantly, compared with Fast-WAM, it improves zero-shot LIBERO-Plus performance by 21.3 percentage points and more than doubles real-world success under visual shifts from 25.8% to 61.5%. These results demonstrate that semantic-temporal modeling effectively complements pixel-generative dynamics for robust manipulation.

</details>


### [207] [Receding-Horizon Next-Best-View Planner for Autonomous Leaf Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28995)
*Arif Ahmed,Sajal K. Das,Parikshit Maini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于质心信息增益（CIG）的下一最佳视角（NBV）规划方法，用于草莓叶片表面重建，在旅行约束下通过滚动时域策略优化视角选择，相比基线方法重建误差降低最高10%。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于机器人视角规划与三维点云重建领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及"plant leaf modeling"和"field deployment"，但这是农业机器人场景，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。唯一可能的借鉴点是其"计算资源受限下的规划优化"思想，但并非面向深度学习模型压缩或加速。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人视角规划与植物点云重建，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。除非对主动采样或信息增益策略有跨领域兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：下一最佳视角（Next-Best-View, NBV）规划，用于植物叶片表面三维重建。应用场景：大规模田间自主机器人重建（field deployment）。传感器类型：点云（point cloud）。目标类别：草莓植株叶片（strawberry plant leaves）。

Model architecture: 提出Centroid-based Information Gain (CIG)函数，用于度量已有点云点相对于质心的空间分布来计算视角效用；并开发滚动时域（receding-horizon）变体，对未来视角进行推理。对比基线为基于可见性信息增益的attention-driven NBV方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。其"轻量化"体现在机器人规划预算和计算资源受限下的视角优化，而非深度学习模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文关注机器人规划预算（robot planning budget）和计算资源（computation resources）限制，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。不涉及星上部署或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用LAST-STRAW公开数据集，包含不同生长阶段的草莓植株点云。评价指标为表面重建误差（surface reconstruction error）和几何保真度（geometric fidelity）。对比方法为attention-driven NBV（基于可见性信息增益）。

Experiments: 在多个生长阶段上验证，滚动时域方法在叶片间遮挡增加时持续降低表面重建误差并提升几何保真度。相比基线方法，重建精度提升最高达10%。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。主要局限包括：1）仅针对植物叶片重建场景，未涉及遥感或卫星数据；2）未提供计算开销、推理速度等量化指标；3）未讨论模型压缩或部署优化；4）仅在单一数据集（LAST-STRAW）上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限，但可提取以下思路：1）"信息增益"（information gain）思想可类比到目标检测中的难例挖掘或主动采样策略，用于在轨场景下优先处理信息量大的区域；2）"计算资源受限下的优化"理念与星上算力受限问题有概念上的相似性，可启发在轨检测中如何平衡推理精度与计算开销；3）滚动时域（receding-horizon）规划思想可类比为在轨检测中的时序推理或帧间信息复用策略。但整体而言，该论文与遥感目标检测轻量化课题的直接关联较弱。

Abstract: Accurate plant leaf modeling is fundamental to downstream tasks such as plant growth monitoring, and phenotyping for yield estimation. Autonomous robotic reconstruction for large-scale field deployment must address limitations on robot planning budget and computation resources while optimizing viewpoint utility for leaf surface reconstruction. Existing approaches either focus on rigid objects, point-cloud coverage or plant reconstruction without fully addressing the system limitations or exploiting task-driven point cloud utility. In this work, we study next-best-view (NBV) planning for leaf surface reconstruction under travel constraints. We develop a novel Centroid-based Information Gain (CIG) function that measures the spatial distribution of observed points relative to the centroid of the existing point cloud to compute viewpoint utility. We also develop a receding-horizon variant that reasons over future viewpoints. To benchmark our work, we use the LAST-STRAW [1] public dataset that includes point clouds of strawberry plants over different growth stages and compare our method with attention-driven NBV [2] that uses a visibility-based information gain approach. The proposed receding-horizon approach consistently reduces surface reconstruction error and improves geometric fidelity across multiple growth stages, especially under increased inter-leaf occlusion. Results demonstrate that our approach is able to visit viewpoints that reduce surface reconstruction error and improves reconstruc-tion accuracy as compared to the baseline by upto 10%.

</details>


### [208] [D-VLC: Decentralized Vision-Language Collaboration for Heterogeneous Embodied Multi-Robot Systems in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29009)
*Yuan Zhou,Ruitong Lin,Shen Wang,Weiqi Gai,Mo zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于通用VLM的异构多机器人系统框架，通过去中心化异步推理、轻量信息共享、能力感知协作和统一动作接口，实现无需任务/机器人特定训练即可生成机器人动作。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于多机器人系统与VLM/LLM结合的方向，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱或多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及object detection、pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等目标检测或模型压缩技术。仅"lightweight information sharing"一词与轻量化有表面关联，但指的是多机器人间的通信信息共享策略，并非模型轻量化。属于泛机器人/泛机器学习方法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于多机器人系统与VLM结合的研究，仅在"轻量信息共享"和"能力感知协作"概念上可能有间接启发，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：异构多机器人协作任务分解与执行；应用场景：多机器人系统（heterogeneous robot swarms）；传感器类型：视觉感知（VLM视觉输入）；目标类别：机器人动作/任务语义，非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 框架包含四个核心组件：去中心化异步推理（decentralized asynchronous reasoning）、轻量信息共享（lightweight information sharing）、能力感知协作（capability aware collaboration）、统一动作接口（unified action interface）。底层使用通用VLM（general purpose VLMs）生成机器人特定动作，由learning free experts执行，无需任务或机器人特定训练。摘要未说明具体VLM架构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要中的"lightweight information sharing"指多机器人之间共享信息的轻量化策略（减少通信开销），并非模型压缩/加速方法。不涉及pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型轻量化技术。摘要未说明。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。论文面向多机器人系统而非星上/边缘部署场景，不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速。摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在"diverse scenarios"（多样场景）和多个VLM上进行，评价指标为成功率（success rate，>70%）和任务完成时间（completion time，相对geometric greedy baseline降低最多55.8%）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在多样场景和多个VLM上验证，成功率超过70%，完成时间相对geometric greedy baseline最多降低55.8%。摘要未提供与具体SOTA方法的详细对比、消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩；2. 摘要未说明VLM推理的计算开销、实时性、资源约束下的可行性；3. 未提供具体数据集、场景细节和与主流方法的系统对比；4. "learning free experts"的泛化能力和鲁棒性缺乏深入验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 借鉴"轻量信息共享"思想：在星上多载荷/多星协同场景中，可考虑设计轻量化的特征/检测结果共享协议，减少星间通信带宽；2. 借鉴"能力感知协作"（capability aware collaboration）思路：在异构星上计算资源（不同算力NPU/FPGA）间进行任务分配，让不同算力节点执行不同复杂度模型；3. 借鉴"统一动作接口"设计：为不同硬件平台（FPGA/NPU/GPU）设计统一的推理接口，便于模型跨平台部署。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为论文不涉及遥感目标检测和模型压缩核心技术。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems, particularly heterogeneous robot swarms, can improve the efficiency of complex task execution through parallel collaboration and complementary capabilities. However, conventional rule-based methods rely on predefined task models and specialized decision making programs, making it difficult to understand complex semantic instructions and coordinate heterogeneous robots. LLMs introduce strong language understanding and task reasoning capabilities, allowing multi-robot systems to interpret instructions, decompose tasks, and assign roles according to task semantics. VLMs further incorporate visual perception, enabling robots to reason about objects, regions, and spatial relationships in physical environments. Nevertheless, existing LLM/VLM based methods often depend on known maps, centralized and synchronized decision making, limiting their generalization to heterogeneous robots and unseen tasks. We therefore propose a framework that combines decentralized asynchronous reasoning, lightweight information sharing, capability aware collaboration, and a unified action interface, enabling general purpose VLMs to generate robot specific actions executed by learning free experts without task or robot specific training. Experiments across diverse scenarios and multiple VLMs show success rates above 70\%, with completion time reduced by up to 55.8\% relative to the geometric greedy baseline.

</details>


### [209] [DART: Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction for Off-Road Vehicle Jumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29011)
*Yu Hu,Fangzhou Zhao,Mingyuan Sang,Cheng Min,Liang Chen,Wei Li,Wenyu Kuang,Shican Chen,Jinwei Li,Baolei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DART（Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction）方法，用于高速越野车辆空中飞行阶段的俯仰/横滚姿态控制，通过可达性分析给出起飞前可行速度整形律和飞行中基于车轮反作用力矩的姿态调节策略。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。论文研究对象是高速越野车辆（off-road vehicle）的空中飞行阶段姿态控制，属于车辆动力学与机器人控制领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及object detection、remote sensing、pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、FLOPs等任何目标检测或模型轻量化内容。唯一可能的间接关联是"airborne"一词，但此处指车辆腾空而非航空遥感平台。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为越野车辆空中姿态控制，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及object detection、remote sensing、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何核心要素。仅可作为控制理论/可达性分析方法的泛读参考，不建议投入精读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：高速越野车辆空中飞行阶段的俯仰/横滚姿态控制（pitch/roll control during airborne phase）。场景：越野地形（crests, ledges, ditches, banked run-ups）高速行驶导致车辆腾空后的姿态恢复。传感器类型：摘要未说明（推测为车辆IMU/轮速传感器）。目标类别：不适用（非检测任务）。

Model architecture: DART（Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction）框架，包含：1) 基于可达性分析（reachability analysis）的闭式可行起飞集合（certified feasible-takeoff set），用于go/no-go判断和起飞前速度整形律（pre-takeoff speed-shaping law）；2) 飞行中基于转向解耦车轮反作用力矩（steer-resolved wheel-reaction torque）的俯仰/横滚调节；3) 基于偏航耦合分析（yaw-coupling analysis）的每飞行横滚闩锁（per-flight roll latch）。对比基线：reaction-wheel-style PD (RW-PD) 和 time-optimal bang-bang (TOBB)。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。其核心是控制律设计与可达性分析，而非深度学习模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。所有结果来自BeamNG.tech确定性全尺寸仿真（deterministic full-scale simulation），硬件验证（hardware validation）尚未开展。控制律本身为闭式解析形式（closed-form），计算开销可能较低，但摘要未给出量化数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：无标准数据集，使用BeamNG.tech确定性全尺寸仿真平台。平台：1383 kg越野车辆。评价指标：1) 触地速度降低百分比（touchdown speed reduction 36%）；2) 安全着陆成功率（on-target landings，0/30提升至30/30；DART在steep-lip场景下29/30 vs RW-PD和TOBB的0/30）；3) 中位俯仰误差（median pitch error ≤2°）；4) 俯仰率恢复上限（pitch-rate budget，9-13°/s至16-18°/s）。

Experiments: 主要实验：1) 在1383 kg平台上量化车轮角动量预算对可恢复俯仰率变化的限制（nose-up方向约9-13°/s，制动方向约2倍，驱动到传动极限时提升至16-18°/s）；2) 校准的起飞前速度调节器将触地速度降低36%，目标着陆从0/30提升至30/30；3) 在steep-lip场景下，DART空中控制律在防撞约束下完成29/30安全着陆，而RW-PD和TOBB均为0/30；4) 在倾斜跑道（banked run-ups）上，DART在所有横坡下保持中位俯仰误差≤2°，在γ=12°时基线分离最大；5) 横滚闩锁在低扰动场景保持纯俯仰分配，在横滚受限时启用双轴控制。所有结果均为仿真，硬件验证未开展。

Limitations: 1) 所有结果来自仿真（BeamNG.tech），硬件验证（hardware validation）仍待开展，实际部署效果未知；2) 研究对象为越野车辆而非遥感卫星或航空平台，与本研究方向无直接关联；3) 摘要未说明控制律的计算复杂度、实时性量化指标；4) 未涉及任何深度学习模型、目标检测或模型压缩内容；5) 未报告能耗、算力需求等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，但仍有少量方法论层面的间接借鉴：1) "reachability-gated"（可达性门控）思想——在资源受限的星上环境中，可借鉴"先判断可行性再执行"的思路，例如在推理前根据输入图像质量或目标尺度分布决定是否启用高精度重检测分支，实现自适应计算分配；2) 闭式解析解（closed-form）设计思路——在星上算力受限条件下，将部分计算转化为闭式/解析形式可降低推理开销，这与轻量化网络设计中的"减少冗余计算"理念相通；3) 约束反向传播（back-propagate constraint）思想——将部署约束（如内存、延迟、能耗上限）反向传播到模型设计阶段，类似"硬件感知NAS"（hardware-aware NAS）的思路。但整体而言，本文对课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Traversing crests, ledges, and ditches at high speed often launches vehicles into the air, and a mishandled landing presents a substantial crash hazard. We show that the airborne phase is barely controllable: on a 1383 kg platform the wheel angular-momentum budget caps the recoverable pitch-rate change at roughly $9$-$13^\circ$/s in the tighter nose-up direction under drive at typical takeoff wheel speeds, and at about twice that in the reverse-inclusive braking direction; driving the wheels to their drivetrain hard limit raises the measured nose-up ceiling to only $16$-$18^\circ$/s. Takeoff pitch-rate disturbances beyond this directional budget are physically unrecoverable in flight, so the decisive leverage lies before takeoff. DART (Dual-Axis Airborne Reachability-Gated Torque-Reaction) back-propagates the landing constraint into a closed-form certified feasible-takeoff set, which supplies a conservative go/no-go condition and a pre-takeoff speed-shaping law. In flight, DART regulates pitch and roll via steer-resolved wheel-reaction torque, governed by a per-flight roll latch derived from the yaw-coupling analysis. In deterministic full-scale simulation in BeamNG.tech, a calibrated pre-takeoff speed regulator reduces touchdown speed by 36% and raises on-target landings from 0/30 to 30/30. Under the same steep-lip approach the airborne law completes 29/30 safe landings under crash-avoidance bounds versus 0/30 for reaction-wheel-style PD (RW-PD) and time-optimal bang-bang (TOBB). On banked run-ups DART holds the median pitch error at or below $2^\circ$ at every cross-slope, with the largest baseline separation at $γ=12^\circ$. Across disturbance regimes, the latch preserves pitch-only allocation on low-disturbance entries and enables dual-axis control when roll becomes binding. All results are from simulation; hardware validation remains open.

</details>


### [210] [Auto-JEPA: A Latent World Model of Continuous Intent for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29031)
*Jiwei Yang,Zhengxian Chen,Chaosheng Huang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Auto-JEPA，一种面向自动驾驶的action-oriented潜在世界模型，通过joint-embedding prediction预测未来驾驶意图嵌入，无需重建完整未来世界即可实现高质量规划。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文属于自动驾驶/世界模型领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。虽然涉及视觉感知和预测，但场景是自动驾驶（autonomous driving），传感器是车载视觉，目标任务是驾驶规划（planning），而非遥感目标检测。轻量化方面仅提到"frozen visual encoder"和"optimizing only task-specific modules"，属于参数冻结策略，与模型压缩/剪枝/量化等轻量化方法不同。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于自动驾驶世界模型/规划领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。虽然"冻结编码器+仅训练任务模块"和"预测意图而非重建完整世界"的思想有一定启发，但整体借鉴价值有限，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶未来驾驶意图预测与轨迹规划（planning）；应用场景：自动驾驶（autonomous driving）；传感器类型：车载视觉观测（visual observations）；目标类别：动态车辆、驾驶场景中的相关目标。不涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱等。

Model architecture: Auto-JEPA采用joint-embedding prediction架构：给定视觉观测、自车运动历史（egomotion history）和导航指令（navigation commands），预测与未来自车轨迹潜在表示对齐的意图嵌入（intent embedding）。预测的意图从固定轨迹记忆库（fixed trajectory memory）中检索可执行轨迹，再由场景条件候选选择模块（scene-conditioned candidate selection module）排序。视觉编码器保持冻结（frozen），无需显式感知标注，不使用学习式轨迹生成器。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确采用传统轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）。仅提到"keeps the visual encoder frozen"（冻结视觉编码器）和"optimizing only task-specific modules"（仅优化任务特定模块），这是一种参数高效训练策略，可减少可训练参数量，但并非面向部署的模型压缩方法。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然冻结编码器+仅训练任务模块的策略在训练阶段节省计算，但未讨论推理阶段的部署可行性或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NAVSIM v1和NAVSIM v2（自动驾驶仿真数据集）；评价指标：PDMS（91.3 on NAVSIM v1）和EPDMS（89.1 on NAVSIM v2）。此外进行了语义遮挡实验（semantic occlusion experiments），通过遮挡动态代理区域和影响未来驾驶的车辆来验证意图预测的有效性。

Experiments: 主要结果：NAVSIM v1上达到91.3 PDMS，NAVSIM v2上达到89.1 EPDMS。语义遮挡实验显示：遮挡动态代理区域导致的平均意图变化是等面积随机遮挡的2.97倍；遮挡影响未来驾驶的车辆会显著改变预测意图和所选轨迹，而遮挡不影响驾驶的车辆则几乎不改变结果。这验证了意图预测能引导模型关注规划相关的视觉特征。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，属于自动驾驶规划领域；2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、推理速度等部署指标；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或轻量化方法；4. 未讨论在边缘设备或嵌入式平台上的部署可行性；5. 实验仅在自动驾驶仿真数据集NAVSIM上验证，未涉及遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景不同，但有以下可借鉴思路：1. "joint-embedding prediction"思想——在遥感在轨目标检测中，可考虑预测目标检测的"意图/语义嵌入"而非完整重建场景，减少计算开销；2. 冻结backbone（frozen visual encoder）+仅训练任务特定模块的策略，可类比于遥感模型中的backbone冻结+轻量检测头设计，有助于在轨部署时减少可训练参数和存储；3. 场景条件候选选择（scene-conditioned candidate selection）思想可类比于遥感目标检测中的候选区域筛选/稀疏化策略，减少无效计算。

Abstract: Existing autonomous-driving world models typically perform dense prediction of future videos, occupancy states, BEV representations, or agent motion. We argue that planning need not reconstruct the complete future world, but only focus on scene features that affect future ego action. Based on this perspective, we propose Auto-JEPA, an action-oriented latent world model that learns continuous future driving intent through joint-embedding prediction. Given visual observations, egomotion history, and navigation commands, Auto-JEPA predicts an intent embedding aligned with the latent representation of the future ego trajectory. The predicted intent retrieves executable trajectories from a fixed trajectory memory, which are then ranked by a scene-conditioned candidate selection module. Auto-JEPA keeps the visual encoder frozen, requires no explicit perception annotations, and uses no learned trajectory generator. By optimizing only task-specific modules for trajectory representation, intent prediction, and candidate selection, Auto-JEPA achieves 91.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. Semantic occlusion experiments show that masking dynamic-agent regions induces an average intent change 2.97x that of equal-area random masking. Moreover, occluding vehicles that affect future driving substantially changes the predicted intent and selected trajectory, whereas both remain essentially unchanged when non-influential vehicles are occluded. These results show that future-intent prediction encourages the model to focus on planning-relevant visual features and supports high-quality planning without dense future-world modeling.

</details>


### [211] [Outcome-Guided Distillation: A Teacher-Student Framework to Advance VLM Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29052)
*Zeyu Dong,Yimin Zhu,Yu Wu,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于VLM反思推理的教师-学生蒸馏框架，将结构化推理与几何精度结合用于端到端自动驾驶，提升可解释性与驾驶性能。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文聚焦端到端自动驾驶（autonomous driving），并非遥感/卫星场景；虽然涉及teacher-student知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）和推理效率优化，但蒸馏对象是VLM推理能力而非目标检测模型，且未涉及遥感目标检测或星上部署。属于泛机器学习/泛视觉方法，对轻量化蒸馏思想有一定借鉴意义，但与遥感在轨目标检测方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，蒸馏思想有一定借鉴价值但场景差异大，且缺乏部署指标。可作为知识蒸馏方法综述性参考，不建议作为核心参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：端到端自动驾驶（E2E autonomous driving），从视觉观测直接映射到控制动作。应用场景：自动驾驶复杂场景下的可解释推理与驾驶预测。传感器类型：视觉观测（visual observations），具体传感器类型摘要未说明。目标类别：非目标检测任务，而是驾驶控制与轨迹预测（waypoint prediction）。

Model architecture: 教师-学生架构（teacher-student architecture）。教师模型：VLM生成逻辑解释并进行反思性推理（reflective reasoning），在ground-truth action监督下迭代精炼推理；学生模型：通过监督微调（supervised fine-tuning）蒸馏教师推理能力。另设计独立的waypoint decoder，将文本推理转化为连续轨迹。整体采用staged inference engine（分阶段推理引擎）整合推理与驾驶预测两个目标。

Lightweight method: 采用知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）思想：教师VLM生成推理文本，学生模型通过supervised fine-tuning蒸馏教师的推理能力，从而在不依赖中间标签（intermediate labels）的情况下实现零样本泛化。蒸馏目标是推理能力而非模型压缩，未涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、NAS或参数量/FLOPs优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及"inference efficiency"和"deployable systems"，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。摘要未说明是否面向边缘/车载/星上部署。整体部署可行性信息不足。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Waymo benchmarks（Waymo自动驾驶基准）。评价指标：zero-shot reasoning（零样本推理）、waypoint accuracy（路径点精度）、inference efficiency（推理效率）。具体数值除约24%性能提升外，摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Waymo benchmarks上与经典基于推理的baseline对比，在zero-shot reasoning、waypoint accuracy和inference efficiency上均优于baseline。消融实验验证了推理文本对驾驶推理的显著贡献：相比不含推理的相同模型，性能提升约24%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关，场景差异大；2. 蒸馏对象是VLM推理能力而非检测模型，轻量化方法不可直接迁移；3. 未提供模型规模、参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署关键指标；4. 未涉及遥感图像（optical/SAR/高光谱/多光谱）或遥感目标类别（舰船/飞机/车辆）；5. 摘要未说明教师-学生蒸馏对模型压缩的实际效果。

Ideas for my research: 1. 教师-学生蒸馏框架中"反思性推理"（reflective reasoning）的思想可借鉴：在遥感目标检测中，可设计教师模型对检测结果进行反思性精炼，再蒸馏给学生轻量模型，提升小目标检测精度；2. 无中间标签的蒸馏方式（在ground-truth监督下精炼）对遥感标注稀缺场景有启发，可减少对精细标注的依赖；3. staged inference engine（分阶段推理）思想可用于遥感在轨检测的"粗筛-精检"两阶段轻量化设计；4. 但需注意：该论文的蒸馏目标是语言推理，而遥感检测蒸馏目标是特征/检测头，迁移时需要重新设计蒸馏损失。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving aims to learn a direct mapping from visual observations to control actions. However, these E2E models often act as black boxes and struggle with complex scenarios. To address this, recent works incorporate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide explicit reasoning, enhancing both interpretability and driving robustness. These approaches typically rely on pre-generated annotations, which suffer from potentially flawed labels and require costly human labor. In this work, we propose a new framework that integrates structured reasoning and geometric precision through a teacher-student architecture. The teacher model introduces reflective reasoning, where the VLM generates logical explanations and then reflectively refines the reasoning under the supervision of ground-truth action. This enhances zero-shot generalization without intermediate labels. The student model distills the teacher's reasoning capabilities via supervised fine-tuning. We also design a separate waypoint decoder that interprets textual reasoning into continuous trajectories. Our proposed solution integrates two goals: providing explicit reasoning for interpretability and delivering robust and accurate driving performance. It leverages the synergy between these two objectives within a staged inference engine to enhance driving performance and explicitly uses the reasoning to guide driving prediction. Evaluated on Waymo benchmarks, our framework outperforms classical reasoning-based baselines in zero-shot reasoning, waypoint accuracy, and inference efficiency. Our experiments validate this design, demonstrating that the reasoning text makes a significant contribution to driving inference, resulting in around a 24% improvement in performance compared to an identical model that lacks reasoning. Our work advances reasoning-driven autonomous driving toward interpretable and deployable systems.

</details>


### [212] [VSTaI: Design and Characterization of Variable-Stiffness Tactile Interfaces Based on 3D-Printed Structured Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29102)
*Yiting Mo,Xinyuan Mao,Jashan Preet Singh,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于真空诱导3D打印结构化织物堵塞的可变刚度触觉界面（VSTaI），用于触诊训练中软组织刚度变化的实时渲染。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文属于触觉反馈/人机交互/医疗模拟训练领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为医疗触觉模拟训练中的可变刚度触觉界面，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：可变刚度触觉界面设计与评估；应用场景：医疗触诊训练模拟器（physical examination training）；传感器类型：不适用（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文描述的是硬件装置而非深度学习模型：真空密封织物层夹在两层硅胶之间，通过调节内部压力调控刚度；测试了四种不同几何参数的织物图案。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。其"compact"指硬件装置紧凑性，与模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署指标。论文中的"real time"指触觉渲染的实时性，与在轨推理实时性无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：不适用（非数据驱动方法）。评价指标：力-压痕测试（force-indentation tests）、有效模量（effective modulus）、刚度提升百分比（stiffness increase up to 140%）、刚度空间均匀性（spatial uniformity of tactile stiffness）。实验设置：大气压与真空两种条件下的力-压痕测试。

Experiments: 主要结果：真空堵塞使刚度显著提升，有效模量从亚兆帕提升到兆帕级别；一种配置在堵塞状态下刚度提升高达140%；圆形锁子甲图案（circular chainmail patterns）提供最均匀的触觉刚度空间分布；更密集的几何结构达到更高的峰值刚度；VSTaI对底层几何形状表现出优异的贴合性（conformability）。

Limitations: 摘要未说明具体局限性。但该论文与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化方向完全无关，属于硬件触觉界面设计，无法为在轨目标检测模型轻量化提供直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题（遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化）基本无直接可借鉴点。若强行寻找关联，其"variable-stiffness"和"compact"的硬件设计思路或可类比于在轨硬件资源受限条件下的自适应计算分配思想，但这是非常牵强的类比，实际借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Realistic palpation training requires reliable rendering of soft tissue stiffness changes in real time, which is difficult to achieve with conventional simulators. This paper presents a compact, variable-stiffness tactile interface (VSTaI) based on vacuum-induced jamming of 3D-printed structured fabrics. A vacuum-sealed fabric layer is sandwiched between two silicone layers, and stiffness is tuned by regulating internal pressure. Four fabric patterns with different geometric parameters were fabricated and evaluated using force-indentation tests under atmospheric and vacuum conditions. Across the tested pattern and geometry combinations, vacuum jamming increased stiffness significantly, producing an effective modulus from sub-megapascal to megapascal levels. Specifically, one configuration exhibited a stiffness increase of up to 140% under the jammed state. Circular chainmail patterns provided the most spatially uniform distribution of tactile stiffness, while denser geometries reached higher peak stiffness. VSTaI was also shown to exhibit excellent conformability to the underlying geometry. These results support structured-fabric jamming as a practical approach for shape-conformable, tunable-stiffness displays aimed at physical examination training.

</details>


### [213] [ActFovea: Runtime Safeguarding for VLA Policies via Spatiotemporal Visual-Action Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29169)
*Wenda Yu,Tianshi Wang,Fengling Li,Xin Li,Jingjing Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActFovea 是一个面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）机器人操控策略的即插即用运行时安全防护框架，通过构建动作条件化的注视区域并检测时空视觉-动作一致性来识别和缓解运行时扰动，无需重训练或修改底层策略。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于机器人操控（robotic manipulation）领域的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略安全防护研究，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景完全无关。虽然涉及视觉感知、目标区域关注（foveated regions）等概念，但既不是遥感目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理或边缘/星上部署。其核心贡献是运行时安全防护（runtime safeguarding）机制，属于泛机器人视觉方法，对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的借鉴价值有限。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于机器人操控领域的 VLA 策略运行时安全防护研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。虽然"动作条件化注视区域"和"时空一致性检测"等抽象概念有一定启发，但缺乏遥感场景、目标检测任务和轻量化方法等核心要素，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：VLA（Vision-Language-Action）机器人操控策略的运行时安全防护（runtime safeguarding）。应用场景：机器人操控（robotic manipulation），具体在 LIBERO 基准套件上评估。传感器类型：视觉观测（visual observations）、机器人运动学（robot kinematics）、本体感受状态（proprioceptive states）、动作序列（action chunks）。目标类别：非遥感目标，涉及机器人操控中的接触相关区域（contact-relevant areas）和预测运动走廊（predicted motion corridors）。

Model architecture: ActFovea 是一个即插即用（plug-and-play）的防护框架，不修改底层 VLA 策略。其关键模块包括：(1) 动作条件化注视区域构建（action-conditioned foveated regions），利用机器人运动学、本体感受状态和近期动作来保留接触相关区域和预测运动走廊，同时抑制任务无关视觉内容；(2) 运行时风险检测模块，评估视觉运动与观测新鲜度是否与几何、本体感受和动作转换保持一致；(3) 扰动特定候选观测构建与恢复验证模块，仅在验证动作块后接受恢复；(4) 有界安全失败过程（bounded safe-failure procedure），当观测陈旧或重放导致无法可靠恢复时触发。底层策略为 π₀。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。该论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。ActFovea 是附加在 VLA 策略之上的防护模块，其设计目标是运行时安全而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关指标。虽然 ActFovea 被描述为"即插即用"且不重训练底层策略，但未提供任何计算开销或部署可行性数据。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO 基准套件（多个套件，closed-loop evaluations）。底层策略：π₀。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate），具体包括：(1) 局部视觉叠加（localized visual overlays）下成功率从 49.3% 提升到 90.3%，缩小了与干净性能差距的 93.7%；(2) 动作漂移（action drift）下提升 7.0 个百分点；(3) 视觉延迟（visual delay）下提升 9.8 个百分点；(4) 冻结观测重放（frozen-observation replay）下所有试验均触发及时安全失败，无未受保护失败。同时保持干净任务（clean-task）性能。

Experiments: 在 LIBERO 多个套件上对 π₀ 进行闭环评估。主要结果：(1) 局部视觉叠加场景下成功率从 49.3% 提升至 90.3%，填补了与干净性能差距的 93.7%；(2) 动作漂移场景提升 7.0 个百分点；(3) 视觉延迟场景提升 9.8 个百分点；(4) 冻结观测重放场景下所有试验均触发及时安全失败，无未受保护失败；(5) 干净任务性能得到保持。摘要未说明消融实验细节或与其它防护方法的对比。

Limitations: (1) 该论文完全面向机器人操控场景，与遥感/卫星目标检测无直接关联；(2) 摘要未说明 ActFovea 的计算开销、参数量或推理延迟，无法判断其是否适合资源受限的星上部署；(3) 摘要未说明方法对传感器类型（如 SAR、高光谱、多光谱）的适用性；(4) 仅在 LIBERO 基准上评估，泛化性未知；(5) 摘要未说明安全失败过程的具体实现细节和边界条件。

Ideas for my research: 尽管该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但仍有少量可借鉴的抽象思路：(1) "动作条件化注视区域"（action-conditioned foveated regions）的思想——在遥感场景中可类比为"任务条件化的感兴趣区域裁剪"，即根据轨道参数、姿态信息或先验目标位置动态裁剪输入图像，减少送入检测网络的数据量，从而降低计算开销，这与在轨轻量化推理中的"注意力引导裁剪"思路相通；(2) "时空一致性检测"（spatiotemporal consistency）思想——在遥感在轨场景中可类比为检测帧间一致性异常（如传感器故障、数据重放、时间戳错乱），用于在轨系统的鲁棒性保障；(3) "有界安全失败"（bounded safe-failure）思想——在星上推理中，当检测置信度过低或输入数据异常时，主动触发降级/安全模式而非盲目输出，这对在轨系统的可靠性设计有参考价值。但整体而言，该论文对"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain vulnerable to runtime disturbances that break the temporal alignment among visual observations, robot states, and executed actions. We introduce ActFovea, a plug-and-play safeguarding framework that detects and mitigates such failures without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA policy. ActFovea uses robot kinematics, proprioceptive states, and recent actions to construct action-conditioned foveated regions that retain contact-relevant areas and predicted motion corridors while suppressing task-irrelevant visual content. It detects runtime risks by evaluating whether visual motion and observation freshness remain consistent with geometric, proprioceptive, and action transitions. For recoverable disturbances, ActFovea constructs disturbance-specific candidate observations and accepts a recovery only after verifying the resulting action chunk. When stale or replayed observations make reliable recovery impossible, it invokes a bounded safe-failure procedure. In closed-loop evaluations of $π_0$ across multiple LIBERO suites, ActFovea increases success under localized visual overlays from 49.3\% to 90.3\%, closing 93.7\% of the gap to clean performance. It further improves success under action drift and visual delay by 7.0 and 9.8 percentage points, respectively, while preserving clean-task performance. Under frozen-observation replay, ActFovea triggers timely safe failure in all trials, with no unprotected failures. These results demonstrate that spatiotemporal visual-action consistency provides an effective basis for runtime safeguarding of VLA policies.

</details>


### [214] [CLIFT: Turning Gemini Robotics On-Device into Humanoid Specialists via Non-Invasive Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29172)
*Yuxin Chen,Hari Srikanth,Nathan Jew,Menglin Wu,Pengcheng Wang,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Peng Xu,Jinyu Xie,Thomas Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在封闭权重机器人基础模型的托管SFT API范式下，如何通过CLIFT（Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning）方法实现闭环策略改进，将部署时的奖励反馈转化为API兼容的监督数据，从而在不访问模型权重、梯度或内部信号的情况下，推动人形机器人完成敏捷接触丰富的操作任务。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。本文属于机器人基础模型（robot foundation models）与闭环微调（closed-loop fine-tuning）方向，核心场景是人形机器人操作（humanoid manipulation），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱等遥感场景完全无关。虽然涉及"部署"（deployment settings）和"模型适配"（model adaptation），但这里的部署指机器人本体部署，而非星上/边缘部署；也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化（pruning/quantization/distillation/NAS等）。唯一可借鉴的是"在无法访问模型内部的情况下进行模型适配"这一思想，与在轨场景中可能遇到的"黑盒模型适配"问题有间接类比价值，但整体相关性很弱。

Reading priority: 1分。本文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于机器人基础模型微调领域。虽然"黑盒适配"思想有一定类比价值，但缺乏遥感场景、目标检测和轻量化方法等核心要素，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人敏捷操作策略微调（humanoid manipulation policy adaptation）；应用场景：封闭权重机器人基础模型的托管SFT API部署场景；传感器类型：摘要未说明（可能涉及机器人本体传感器，但未明确）；目标类别：人形机器人操作任务（agile, contact-rich manipulation），非遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 基础模型为Gemini Robotics On-Device (GROD)，一个封闭权重的机器人基础模型；对比模型为一个领先的开源权重VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型。提出的CLIFT方法本身不是新的网络架构，而是一种训练/微调策略，将部署时的奖励反馈转化为API兼容的监督数据。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。核心关注点是"封闭权重模型的黑盒适配"，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文讨论的"部署"（deployment settings）指机器人本体的部署环境（novel states, action tracking dynamics, latency, controller-specific failure modes），而非星上/边缘计算部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。实验在真实人形机器人（real humanoid）上开展，使用GROD模型进行托管API适配。评价指标为任务成功率（success rate），文中提到经过两个flywheel cycles后达到near-perfect success。对比基线为在相同演示数据上训练的开源权重VLA模型。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：(1) 通过API直接SFT显著优于在相同演示数据上训练的开源权重VLA模型；(2) 直接SFT在敏捷接触丰富任务上仍达不到部署级掌握水平；(3) 提出的CLIFT方法通过将部署时奖励反馈转化为API兼容监督数据，实现闭环策略改进，两个flywheel cycles后达到接近完美的成功率，且全程无需"打开模型盒子"（不访问权重、梯度、似然或损失）。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断的局限包括：(1) 仅针对机器人操作场景，不涉及遥感或视觉目标检测；(2) 依赖托管API的可用性和数据提交格式；(3) 未提供计算资源、训练成本等量化信息；(4) 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署约束；(5) 实验规模（任务种类、机器人数量）摘要未说明。

Ideas for my research: 尽管本文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但有一个思想值得借鉴：在无法访问模型内部（权重/梯度）的情况下进行模型适配。在星上部署场景中，如果使用商业封闭权重的遥感基础模型（如某些闭源大模型API），可能面临类似的"黑盒适配"问题——无法直接在轨微调。CLIFT将部署时反馈转化为监督数据的思路，可以类比为"将星上推理结果与地面真值之间的差异转化为可提交的微调数据"，用于在轨或地面闭环迭代优化。不过这一借鉴较为间接，且遥感在轨场景通常更关注模型轻量化本身（剪枝/量化/蒸馏），而非黑盒适配。

Abstract: While robot foundation models are growing increasingly capable, the strongest models are typically trained on proprietary data and remain closed-source, limiting downstream users' ability to adapt them to new tasks, embodiments, and deployment settings. Following the LLM community, an emerging access paradigm for closed-weight robot foundation models is the managed supervised fine-tuning (SFT) API, where users submit training data and receive a tuned policy without access to model weights, gradients, or training internals. While such APIs let downstream users leverage powerful proprietary foundation models, they restrict policy improvement to pure imitation, ruling out reinforcement learning and other closed-loop methods that rely on internal training signals. This limitation is particularly acute for agile, contact-rich humanoid manipulation, where the gap between policy outputs and deployed behavior is large due to novel states, action tracking dynamics, latency, and controller-specific failure modes. We study how effective this managed-API regime is for humanoid adaptation, and how closed-loop improvement can be realized within it to push policies toward task mastery. We conduct one of the first empirical studies of managed-API adaptation on a real humanoid, instantiated on Gemini Robotics On-Device (GROD). We find that direct SFT through the API substantially outperforms a leading open-weight VLA trained on the same demonstrations, yet still falls short of deployment-level mastery on agile, contact-rich tasks. To close this gap, we introduce CLIFT: Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning, which turns deployment-time reward feedback into API-compatible supervised data and enables closed-loop policy improvement without accessing weights, gradients, likelihoods, or losses-pushing GROD to near-perfect success after two flywheel cycles, all without "opening the model box."

</details>


### [215] [MROPE: A Multi-Robot Safe Cooperative Strategy via combined Predictive Safety Filters and Ellipse-based Constraint Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29203)
*Alice Rosetti,Lorenzo Pichierri,Domenico Cappello,Fabrizio Schiano,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MROPE分层策略，将无人机集群协同追踪动态目标的任务与局部安全约束解耦，通过动态聚合障碍物几何为安全包围椭圆，结合分布式聚合优化、去中心化共识和预测安全滤波器实现实时避障与高效协同。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文涉及无人机集群（drone swarm）目标追踪（target tracking），但并非遥感卫星场景，不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱等遥感数据，也不涉及目标检测模型或模型轻量化。属于机器人/多智能体协同控制与路径规划领域，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于无人机集群协同控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感数据、目标检测模型或模型压缩技术，阅读价值很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无人机集群协同追踪动态目标（cooperative target tracking）；场景：杂乱环境（cluttered environments）中的实时避障与安全监控；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：动态目标（具体类别未说明）。

Model architecture: MROPE分层架构，包含三个层次：1) 高层：分布式聚合优化（distributed aggregative optimization）用于集群协同；2) 中层：去中心化共识方案（decentralized consensus scheme）用于安全区域计算；3) 底层：局部预测安全滤波器（Predictive Safety Filters, PSF）用于实时碰撞避免。核心思想是将复杂障碍物几何动态聚合为单个安全包围椭圆（safe bounding ellipse）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。论文的"轻量化"体现在计算层面：通过将复杂障碍物几何聚合为单个安全椭圆来降低计算复杂度，属于算法层面的计算简化，而非神经网络模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提及"superior real-time efficiency and scalability compared to centralized approaches"，强调实时效率和可扩展性优于集中式方法，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化指标。部署平台为无人机（drone）而非卫星，且未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署细节。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及虚拟实验（Virtual experiments）和真实世界实验（real-world experiments），但未说明具体数据集名称、评价指标（如追踪精度、避障成功率、计算延迟等）的具体数值。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明通过虚拟和真实世界实验验证了框架，展示了相比集中式方法更优的实时效率和可扩展性。未给出具体对比方法、消融实验和量化结果。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何遥感数据或检测模型；2) 不涉及神经网络模型压缩/加速；3) 摘要未提供任何量化性能指标；4) 场景为无人机集群协同控制，与在轨卫星部署场景差异巨大（通信延迟、计算资源、环境约束均不同）。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但有以下间接启发：1) "将复杂障碍物几何动态聚合为单个安全椭圆"的计算简化思想，可类比于在目标检测中通过特征聚合降低计算复杂度；2) 分层架构（高层协同+底层实时）的设计思路，可类比于在轨检测中"粗检测+精检测"的两阶段轻量化策略；3) 分布式/去中心化计算思想对多星协同在轨智能处理有一定参考价值。但整体而言，该论文对"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Deploying drone swarms to track a dynamic target in cluttered environments presents severe computational and safety challenges. We propose MROPE, a hierarchical strategy that decouples the cooperative monitoring mission from strict local safety requirements. To overcome the computational bottlenecks typical of dense spaces, our approach dynamically aggregates complex obstacle geometries into a single safe bounding ellipse for each drone. Methodologically, this architecture is realized by combining distributed aggregative optimization for high-level swarm coordination, a decentralized consensus scheme for the safe area computation, and local Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) for real-time collision avoidance. Virtual and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior real-time efficiency and scalability compared to centralized approaches.

</details>


### [216] [Event-Based Upper-Body Humanoid Teleoperation Under Challenging Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29227)
*Haoyu Fu,Zhou Ge,Chengze Li,Chenzhao Sun,Ze Cui,Wenjing Zhou,Xulei Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经形态事件相机（event-based vision）的实时上半身人体到人形机器人运动模仿框架，利用事件相机的高动态范围和低延迟特性，在嵌入式平台上实现23-34ms的光子到动作端到端延迟。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于机器人遥操作/运动模仿领域，核心是神经形态事件相机（neuromorphic event-based vision）驱动的实时人体姿态估计与运动重定向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关系。但事件相机的高动态范围、低延迟、异步感知特性，以及嵌入式平台部署（NVIDIA Booster T1）和实时推理思路，对星上边缘部署的传感器选择与低延迟处理有一定借鉴意义。

Reading priority: 2分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，核心是机器人遥操作与事件相机感知，不涉及遥感数据、目标检测或模型压缩方法。仅嵌入式实时部署和事件相机感知思路有间接参考价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：实时上半身人体到人形机器人运动模仿（human-to-humanoid motion imitation）；应用场景：遥操作/远程操控（teleoperation），涉及HDR高动态范围、低光照（<5 lux）、快速运动等挑战场景；传感器类型：Prophesee EVK4事件相机（neuromorphic event camera，异步、>120dB动态范围）；目标类别：人体上半身姿态/关节（18-DoF人形机器人）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 架构包含两个核心模块：(1) Perception Module（感知模块）：低延迟，采用优化的事件累积（optimized event accumulation）与重力对齐惯性融合（gravity-aligned inertial fusion）；(2) Motion Module（运动模块）：基于TWIST的因果在线运动重定向（causal online kinematic retargeting）。整体为事件流驱动的感知-运动级联架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏或NAS等模型压缩方法。轻量化主要体现在：(1) 采用事件相机替代传统帧式RGB传感器，从感知源头降低数据冗余和计算负担；(2) 在嵌入式平台NVIDIA Booster T1上部署，强调低延迟实时推理。但未说明具体模型压缩或加速技术细节。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台为嵌入式NVIDIA Booster T1和18-DoF人形机器人上半身装置。端到端光子到动作延迟（photon-to-action latency）为23-34ms，满足实时性要求。摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs、显存占用或能耗数据。虽然部署在嵌入式平台，但面向的是机器人遥操作而非星上遥感，部署场景不同。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体公开数据集。实验在自建的人形机器人遥操作装置上进行，评价指标包括端到端延迟（23-34ms）以及与RGB基线方法的对比结果。未提及mAP、IoU等目标检测标准指标。

Experiments: 在NVIDIA Booster T1嵌入式平台和18-DoF人形机器人上半身装置上验证系统，展示23-34ms端到端延迟，并在实验设置下优于RGB基线。结论指出：事件相机在快速运动或低光照遥操作场景下更优，而光照良好的静态场景可能更适合RGB或混合感知。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱或多光谱数据；(2) 不涉及目标检测任务本身，更不涉及小目标检测或旋转框检测；(3) 未提供模型压缩（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）的具体方法细节；(4) 未报告参数量、FLOPs、能耗等部署量化指标；(5) 实验为自建遥操作场景，缺乏标准遥感数据集验证。

Ideas for my research: (1) 事件相机/神经形态感知的异步、高动态范围特性可启发星上传感器选型思考——在轨场景中光照变化剧烈（如晨昏轨道、地影区），事件相机或混合感知方案可能缓解传统帧式成像的动态范围瓶颈；(2) 嵌入式平台（NVIDIA Booster T1）上的低延迟实时推理思路可借鉴，但需替换为更适合星载的NPU/FPGA方案；(3) 事件累积（event accumulation）与惯性融合的轻量感知策略，可类比思考星上多源数据（如姿态、轨道信息）与成像数据的融合处理；(4) 端到端延迟（photon-to-action latency）作为系统级评价指标，可迁移到星上"成像-检测-决策"全链路延迟评估中。总体而言，借鉴价值有限，属于边缘感知与实时部署的间接参考。

Abstract: We present a real-time upper-body human-to-humanoid motion imitation framework driven by neuromorphic event-based vision. This work addresses practical perceptual bottlenecks of conventional frame-based RGB sensors, specifically their difficulty in high dynamic range (HDR) scenes and rapid motions due to fixed integration times. By leveraging the Prophesee EVK4 event camera, which operates asynchronously with high temporal resolution and a dynamic range exceeding 120 dB, our system supports stable tracking in conditions where standard vision pipelines degrade, such as severe backlighting and very low light environments below 5 lux. The architecture integrates a low-latency Perception Module, utilizing optimized event accumulation and gravity-aligned inertial fusion, with a causal Motion Module (TWIST) that performs online kinematic retargeting. We validate the system on an embedded NVIDIA Booster T1 platform and an 18-DoF humanoid upper-body setup, demonstrating an end-to-end photon-to-action latency of 23-34 ms and advantages over RGB baselines under our experimental setup. The results indicate a practical trade-off: events can be preferable for fast or poorly lit upper-body teleoperation, whereas well-lit static scenes may favor RGB or hybrid sensing.

</details>


### [217] [TacPrint: A Wearable Fingertip Tactile Sensor for Human-to-Robot Contact Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29231)
*Yongxi Liu,Chaofan Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Xiangyin Bao,Boyue Zhang,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacPrint可穿戴指尖触觉传感器，通过24个电容taxel与硅胶皮肤突起一一对应，结合real-to-sim-to-real流程估计接触深度图，用于提升机器人抓取和擦拭等接触密集任务中的人-机器人技能迁移可靠性。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2分）。该论文聚焦机器人触觉感知与人-机器人技能迁移，属于机器人操作/触觉传感领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等遥感目标检测任务。也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等模型压缩或部署技术。唯一可借鉴的是其real-to-sim-to-real仿真到真实迁移思路，但这与遥感在轨检测的域适应问题关联也较间接。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于机器人触觉感知领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或模型压缩/部署技术，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人触觉感知与接触深度图估计、人-机器人技能迁移（grasping/wiping）。应用场景：接触密集的机器人操作任务。传感器类型：可穿戴指尖电容式触觉传感器（24个capacitive taxels）。目标类别：接触区域/接触深度图（contact-depth map），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 摘要未详细说明网络结构。核心硬件设计为硅胶皮肤内表面突起与24个电容taxel一一对应，实现局部化电容响应；通过real-to-sim-to-real流程，从24通道电容信号估计35×26的接触深度图。网络模型具体架构（如CNN/MLP等）摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速方法。其"轻量"体现在硬件层面（低成本、可穿戴、24通道低维传感），而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。其部署场景为机器人本体，与在轨遥感卫星部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真生成标签（simulation-generated labels）用于训练；40次受控实验（controlled trials）和37次参考接触区域未被传感边界截断的实验用于评估。评价指标：contact-region RMSE（0.223±0.161 mm）、weighted-centroid error（1.213±2.379 pixels）、IoU（0.829±0.169）、contact-position error（0.250±0.208 mm）、抓取/擦拭成功率（0%→91.67%/90%）、闭环抓取成功率（87.5%/85% vs 67.5%/45%）。

Experiments: 主要实验：1) 仿真标签下接触区域估计精度评估（RMSE、centroid error、IoU）；2) 真实电容输入下网络预测深度在引导校准接触中心处的MAE为0.085±0.057 mm；3) 人-机器人回放中，触觉引导补偿将抓取和擦拭成功率从0%提升至91.67%和90%；4) 闭环抓取中，密集深度反馈在全部测试位置和边缘接触条件下分别达到87.5%和85%成功率，优于raw-taxel反馈的67.5%和45%。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，缺乏遥感场景、目标检测任务和模型压缩方法；2) 摘要未说明网络模型架构、参数量、计算量等模型信息；3) 摘要未说明模型是否考虑实时推理或资源受限部署；4) 部分实验（37/40 trials）因参考接触区域被传感边界截断而排除，存在边界效应问题；5) 触觉传感的real-to-sim-to-real迁移思路虽可借鉴，但与遥感域适应问题场景差异大。

Ideas for my research: 1) real-to-sim-to-real的仿真-真实域迁移思路可类比遥感中的sim-to-real域适应（如合成遥感图像到真实卫星图像的检测模型迁移），但其具体方法（触觉深度估计）与遥感目标检测差异较大；2) 低维传感（24通道）到高分辨率深度图（35×26）的估计思路，在思路上类似遥感中低分辨率/多光谱到高分辨率重建或超分，但技术路线不同；3) 总体而言，对本课题（在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化）的直接借鉴价值有限，仅可作为跨领域方法学参考。

Abstract: Human-centric data collection is emerging as a significant paradigm for robot skill acquisition, but seamlessly integrating low-cost, scalable tactile sensing systems that capture fine-grained fingertip interactions without compromising natural operation remains a key challenge. This reduces the reliability of human-to-robot transfer in contact-rich tasks. In this work, we present TacPrint, a wearable fingertip tactile sensor, where protrusions on the inner surface of the silicone skin are aligned one-to-one with 24 capacitive taxels to enable localized capacitive responses. A real-to-sim-to-real pipeline estimates a 35 $\times$ 26 contact-depth map from 24-channel capacitive signals. Against simulation-generated labels, the model achieved a contact-region RMSE of 0.223 $\pm$ 0.161 mm, a weighted-centroid error of 1.213 $\pm$ 2.379 pixels, and an IoU of 0.829 $\pm$ 0.169. With measured capacitive inputs, the network-predicted depth evaluated at the guide-calibrated contact center showed a mean absolute error of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.057 mm across all 40 controlled trials, while the mean contact-position error was 0.250 $\pm$ 0.208 mm across the 37 trials whose reference contact regions were not truncated by the sensing boundary. In human-to-robot replay, tactile-guided compensation increased grasping and wiping success rates from 0% to 91.67% and 90%, respectively. In closed-loop grasping, dense-depth feedback achieved success rates of 87.5% over all tested positions and 85% under edge-contact conditions, compared with 67.5% and 45% for raw-taxel feedback.

</details>


### [218] [FBFM: A Training-Free Asynchronous Feedback Mechanism for Flow-Matching in World-Action Models Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29235)
*Peize Li,Ruimeng Zhang,Ru Zhang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM)，一种无需训练的推理机制，通过掩码伪逆校正将闭环反馈嵌入到流匹配生成过程中，实现细粒度时间步级别的在线纠错，提升世界-动作模型（WAM）在长时程机器人控制中的可靠性。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于机器人控制/世界模型领域，核心是world-action models (WAMs) 和 flow matching，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关联。唯一可能的借鉴点是其"training-free"推理机制和在线纠错思想，但这属于泛机器学习方法，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联很弱。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于机器人控制/世界模型领域。除非对"training-free在线纠错"这一抽象方法论感兴趣，否则不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长时程机器人控制（long-horizon robot control）、视觉预测（visual evolution prediction）。应用场景：机器人操作（LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0基准）、真实世界机器人观测预测诊断。传感器类型：机器人视觉观测（图像）。目标类别：机器人动作/状态，非遥感目标。摘要未说明遥感、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感传感器相关内容。

Model architecture: 基于世界-动作模型（WAM），包括联合生成型WAM（DreamZero）和阶段式WAM（LingBot-VA）。核心创新是FBFM机制：在flow matching过程中，对条件速度场（conditional velocity field）施加掩码伪逆校正（masked pseudoinverse correction），利用前一个动作块（action chunk）引导下一个动作块生成，并用执行前一块后观测到的图像引导下一帧预测，形成跨块异步闭环。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统轻量化方法（pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等）。论文强调FBFM是"training-free"（无需训练）的推理机制，即不增加训练成本，仅在推理阶段进行在线校正。这与轻量化部署有一定概念上的关联（不增加训练开销），但并未涉及模型压缩、参数量缩减、FLOPs优化或边缘/星上部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然FBFM是training-free机制，理论上推理开销增加有限，但论文未提供任何计算成本或实时性数据。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0基准任务，以及真实世界机器人观测-预测诊断实验。评价指标：任务成功率（success rate，在有利设置下提升超过5%）、观测预测跟踪质量（tracking）。摘要未说明具体指标数值细节。

Experiments: 在DreamZero（联合生成WAM）和LingBot-VA（阶段式WAM）两种模型上评估FBFM。在选定的LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0任务上，有利设置下成功率提升超过5%。真实世界机器人观测-预测诊断显示跟踪性能显著更好。摘要未提供消融实验和详细对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方向基本无关，属于机器人控制领域。2. 摘要未说明计算开销、推理延迟等部署关键指标。3. 仅在"有利设置"下提升超过5%，泛化性和鲁棒性存疑。4. 未涉及模型压缩、加速或边缘部署。5. 摘要未说明FBFM在复杂、高动态场景下的稳定性。

Ideas for my research: 尽管方向不同，但有以下可借鉴的抽象思想：1. "training-free"在线纠错机制——在遥感在轨目标检测中，若模型在轨运行出现漂移（如光照变化、传感器退化），可借鉴这种无需重新训练的在线校正思路，通过观测反馈修正预测。2. 跨块异步反馈（cross-chunk asynchronous loop）——在遥感时序检测中，可利用前一帧/前一块的检测结果作为条件引导当前帧预测，形成闭环校正。3. 掩码伪逆校正（masked pseudoinverse correction）的数学工具——可用于轻量化模型在轨自适应调整的数学框架。但这些仅是方法论层面的抽象借鉴，不涉及遥感目标检测或轻量化本身。

Abstract: Although world-action models (WAMs) enhance long-horizon robot control by predicting visual evolution before acting, long-horizon reliability demands repeated re-grounding in real observations--not recursive rollout. Existing WAMs address this by refreshing history or KV cache with ground-truth data between chunks. However, such chunk-wise feedback operates at a coarse temporal granularity and thus fails to correct prediction errors at the individual time-step level. To address this, we propose Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM), a training-free inference mechanism that pushes re-grounding inside the actively generated chunk. During flow matching, FBFM applies a masked pseudoinverse correction to the conditional velocity field: it leverages the preceding action chunk to guide generation of the next action chunk, and uses the image observed after executing that preceding chunk to guide the next frame prediction. This cross-chunk pairing--where feedback from one chunk arrives in time to shape the next--creates an asynchronous loop that corrects errors without waiting for chunk boundaries. Being training-free, the mechanism improves responsiveness to unexpected events and suppresses drift in long-horizon tasks. We evaluate FBFM on both a joint-generation WAM (DreamZero) and a stage-wise WAM (LingBot-VA). On selected LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0 tasks, it improves success rates by over 5% in favorable settings, and real-world robot observation-prediction diagnostics show notably better tracking. We argue that FBFM offers a new paradigm for fine-grained online correction, bridging open-loop flow generation with closed-loop real-world dynamics.

</details>


### [219] [MDIR: A Task-Manifold Impedance Retargeting Method for Contact-Rich Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29271)
*Liu Jiahao,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR)方法，将固定笛卡尔阻抗命令重参数化为可执行的任务通道变阻抗命令，以降低遥操作演示中的冲击力、脉冲、力变异性和控制器功率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是机器人遥操作中的阻抗控制重定向问题，属于机器人控制/触觉交互领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。既不是遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测，更不涉及模型压缩或部署优化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于机器人阻抗控制领域，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人遥操作（teleoperation）中的阻抗控制重定向（impedance retargeting）。应用场景：平面擦拭（planar wiping）、抓取放置（pick-and-place）、推挤（pushing）。传感器类型：摘要未说明（涉及Franka Panda机械臂）。目标类别：不适用（非检测任务）。

Model architecture: MDIR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 在operational work、exertion、support通道中表示源响应，并带有passive residual complement；(2) 计算可执行的Cartesian-to-Manifold Retargeting (C2M)基线；(3) 应用Manifold-Constrained Parameter Optimization (MPO)选择可行代表。整体属于机器人控制方法，非深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不适用。该论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（如pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、NAS等）。其"retargeting"概念属于机器人控制参数重定向，与模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。虽然涉及闭环执行（closed-loop executions），但这是机器人控制层面的实时性，与在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 实验平台：Franka Panda机械臂。实验任务：planar wiping、pick-and-place、pushing。评价指标：Task Check通过率（15次闭环执行全部通过）、wrist-force peaks、impulse、force variability、nominal controller power。无标准数据集或检测指标（如mAP、IoU等）。

Experiments: 在Franka Panda上进行了三类任务（擦拭、抓取放置、推挤）的实验。完整MDIR控制器在15次闭环执行中全部通过Task Check，并在所有四个激进性指标（wrist-force peaks、impulse、force variability、nominal controller power）上相对固定阻抗演示有所降低。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显与本研究方向无关：不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。其"retargeting"和"manifold decomposition"概念属于机器人控制领域，对遥感在轨检测轻量化没有直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：该论文强调在保持任务通道响应（task-channel response）的前提下优化控制参数，这种"在约束下优化关键性能指标"的思路在模型压缩中（如在保持检测精度的约束下最小化FLOPs/参数量）有哲学层面的相似性，但方法论上无直接可迁移性。

Abstract: Fixed Cartesian impedance makes contact-rich teleoperation demonstrations practical, but gains that secure progress and contact support also determine impact and force variability. We study single-demonstration controller-to-controller impedance retargeting. Given one fixed Cartesian impedance command sequence {K0, D0, xcmd}, Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) deterministically reparameterizes the recorded controller into an executable task-channel variable-impedance command. MDIR targets this local retargeting problem by preserving projected task-channel responses near the demonstrated trajectory. It represents the source response in operational work, exertion, and support channels with a passive residual complement under a control-chain metric, computes an executable Cartesian-to-Manifold Retargeting (C2M) baseline, and applies Manifold-Constrained Parameter Optimization (MPO) to select a feasible representative with lower wrist-force peaks, impulse, force variability, and nominal controller power. Across planar wiping, pick-and-place, and pushing on a Franka Panda, the full MDIR controller passes Task Check in all 15 closed-loop executions and reduces all four aggressiveness metrics relative to the fixed-impedance demonstrations.

</details>
